# HD2 Sabre Squadron port — working notes

## Layout
- `src/` hand-ported clean C++ (MSVC x86 C++17; check with `python _re/tools/build_check.py`)
  - `common/` C_str/C_fstr, C_chunk, config files, file wrappers · `ls3d/` engine interfaces (math, i3d, igraph, rw_data)
  - `game/` module framework, track module, app core (WinMain/main loop), console, pending.h (not-yet-ported declarations)
  - `net/` bit stream
  - `tier1/` GENERATED mechanical translation of every not-yet-ported function (g++ -m32 -fpermissive);
    regenerate with `python _re/tools/fix_tier1.py --rounds 6 --jobs 10` after a fresh export
- `_re/` reverse-engineering state
  - `decomp/` exported decompilation (one JSON per function; refresh with `tools/export_decomp.py --force`)
  - `symbols.json` (generated from `names/*.txt` by `tools/names.py`; `--apply` pushes to Ghidra)
  - `types/*.spec` struct layouts (`tools/apply_ghidra.py --types`), `90_auto.spec` generated by `tools/auto_classes.py`
  - `class_names.json` vtable -> class name (used by auto_classes and the readers)
  - `ported.txt` addresses with a hand-written version (excluded from tier 1)
  - `script_opcodes.json` script command opcodes, `vm_cases.json` VM switch case -> opcodes
  - `file_map.txt`, `port_order.txt` codebase map / bottom-up order

## Reading code
`python _re/tools/ghidra2cpp.py -d ADDR...` or `-d --range LO HI` (dense, names substituted).

## Conventions
- Methods/classes follow the original style: `C_ClassName`, `S_Struct`, PascalCase methods, `m_` members.
- Unknown callee placeholders: `fn_XXXXXX`, `C_Class_XXXXXX`; rename everywhere with `tools/rename.py OLD NEW`.
- Original addresses as `// 0x00XXXXXX` comments on declarations.
- Copy-protection hooks (calls into .meleeTrackerui, indirection tables 0x8ca840/0x8caf40) are dropped.


## More tools
- `anim_names.txt`: the animation file of every id / stance (movement tables of groups 1 and 2), carry
  and activity id and the seat-weapon poses, dumped from H:/HD2_Unpacked/Tables/anim.sav. The file
  names (Czech) say what an animation id is: "dvere" doors, "medik01" healing, "svazuje" tying up,
  "strihaLOOP" cutting, "podrezSTa" a throat cut, "ridic" / "spol" driver / passenger, ...
- `tools/stl_idioms.py` recognises MSVC6 STL instantiations (vector insert/erase/dtor...) -> `names/zz_idioms.txt`
- `tools/gen_script_tables.py` regenerates `src/script/script_opcodes.h` / `script_tables.cpp` from the
  game's command (0x8338a8), function (0x832d60) and keyword (0x835b68) tables (`script_*.json`)
- `tools/casedis.py TABLE COUNT CASE` disassembles one jump-table case (used for the VM switch)
- `build_index.py` adds callees found in the code text (the API caps callees at 50);
  `export_decomp.py` falls back to force_decompile when decompiled_code is cut at 120000 chars
- `tools/absorb.py LO HI` marks helpers only called by ported code (STL instantiations, inline
  accessors) as ported, so tier 1 stops generating them. Run it after porting a subsystem.
- `tools/fetch_types.py` saves every Ghidra structure layout to `ghidra_layouts.json`; gen_tier1 emits
  them into `tier1_types.h` (the decompiler output uses Ghidra's structs, not only our specs).
- After a subsystem: `names.py`, `absorb.py`, `apply_ghidra.py --symbols --only <addrs>`, then
  occasionally `export_decomp.py --force` + `build_index.py` + `fetch_types.py` + `fix_tier1.py`.
- `harness/vehicle_setup/mutfast.py LIST.py --models DIR [--baseline] [NAME ...]` (2026-10-21): the
  vehicle ports' mutation tests on a copy of src/ and the harness (the real sources stay editable
  meanwhile), only the mutated file rebuilt, the models in parallel chunks, a 120 s timeout per chunk:
  about 12 s a mutation (the in-place runner before it rebuilt everything and ran the models one
  after another, 1 min 45 s). The lists of parts 1c-iii-d..1c-iv-b are in
  `harness/vehicle_setup/mutations/` (each entry: file, name, a text found once, its replacement);
  DIR is export_cars.py's output.

- WARNING: Ghidra's /import_data_types replaces every struct that the source references but does
  not define with an empty 1-byte struct (this emptied C_str and I3D_frame once). Use
  `tools/import_spec.py FILE.spec` to push one spec: it appends definitions of every external struct
  copied from Ghidra. Ghidra-side layouts of many auto classes are richer than 90_auto.spec, so a
  full `apply_ghidra.py --types` re-import would lose data. Diff against `ghidra_layouts.json` after.
- WARNING (2026-10-18): the vtable slots' function types are global `/functions/_func_N`, numbered
  from 1 in every parse, and an import replaces types of the same name: every partial import gave
  the vtables imported before it the signatures of its own slots (C_GameState::GetState decompiled
  with the arguments of an ISND_stream method, C_Actor's slots those of the last import). Repaired by
  importing all 170 vtables in one parse and naming each slot type `_func_<vtbl>__<slot>`
  (`tools/name_func_types.py`; import_spec.py and apply_ghidra.py --types now name the new ones
  after every import), then every decompile was exported again. Check: no field type matching
  `_func_\d+ *` in ghidra_layouts.json; gen_tier1 / import_spec match `_func_\w+`.
- `apply_ghidra.vtable_layouts` names pure-virtual (_purecall) slots after a direct subclass's override.

- `tools/ls3d_ghidra.py` turns `src/ls3d/i3d.h` / `igraph.h` (I3DMETHOD / I3D_SLOT / Field<T>)
  into Ghidra structs with typed `__stdcall` vtable function pointers; `apply_ghidra.py --types` appends
  it to the spec import. Keep i3d.h the single source of engine knowledge: name a slot there, re-run
  `apply_ghidra.py --types`, and every call through that slot decompiles with its real arguments.
  (Vtable fields must be inline function pointers; the decompiler ignores typedef'd function types.)
- Porting a subsystem: write `types/NN_x.spec` + `names/x.txt` with full prototypes (this-types!),
  `apply_ghidra.py --types`, `names.py --apply`, `export_decomp.py --force --only <range>`, then read
  `ghidra2cpp.py -d --range` — the decompilation is far cleaner than before typing.
- `names/zz_*.txt` (generated) have the lowest priority in `names.py`.
- `tools/ret_audit.py [module...] | --slots | --src [glob...]` compares declared stack arguments
  with the code's `RET n` (names prototypes, typed vtable slots, C++ declarations with an address
  comment; follows JMP thunks through the member types). Run it after porting a module: Ghidra
  gives thunks and empty virtual stubs no arguments, and the decompiled callers then lose their
  pushes. Known false positives are listed in its docstring. `export_decomp.py` now also falls
  back to force_decompile when get_functions times out (the script VM 0x00416e10).
- `tools/asm.py dis|flow|x87 ADDR [FROM TO]` reads a function's disassembly from Ghidra: annotated
  (callee names, float constants), only its flow (compares, jumps, calls, state stores), or as a
  symbolic x87 stack (each store / compare with its expression, sums grouped as the code adds
  them). Check every state machine, filter loop and float sum with it: the decompile drops jumps
  and regroups sums (controllers phase 4). The docstring decodes the FNSTSW compare + jump pairs.
- `tools/cpp_names.py HEADER... [--range LO-HI]` drafts names-file lines (qualified name + Ghidra
  prototype with explicit `this`) from the address comments of hand-ported C++ headers; review the
  ctors/dtors, scalar-deleting dtors, __fastcall helpers and duplicate addresses by hand (2026-10-19,
  used for the meleeTracker's 183 part-2/3 names). Then `ret_audit.py <module>` checks them.
- `tools/check_ls3d_vtables.py` (after `fetch_types.py`) lists LS3D vtables whose Ghidra copy still has
  old `SlotNN` names after a slot was named in i3d.h, and prints the `import_spec.py --structs` repair;
  then re-export the decompiles that still mention the old names (2026-10-17: 15 stale vtables, ~50
  tier-1 errors).

## Status (2026-10-13)
Ported (src/): track module, module framework, C_chunk, C_str, app core, bit stream, console,
- mission scripts (`src/script/`): compiler, expressions, interpreter + condition functions, actor
  scripts, C_ScriptModule (SaveGame/LoadGame/net state stay tier 1)
- resource caches (`game/resource_cache.*`): C_ResourceCache<T> template with the model, sound,
  animation, shadow and versions caches; AddDataDirectory
- sound (`game/sound_table.*`, `game/sound_module.*`): IngameSounds table, C_SoundModule (effects, slots,
  channels, music streams, sector/portal damping, AI hearing listeners, weapon flash lights)
- destructible objects (`game/versions.*`): C_VersionsModule / C_VersionMaster / C_VersionKeeper,
  damage stages from the "d<model>" versions models, debris physics, collision boxes, save/net state
- particle effects (`game/particles.*`, `common/color.*`): effects.def definitions, S_Particle motion
  phases, C_ParticleEmitter (bursts/continuous emission, depth sort, quad building with HSV brightness),
  particle pool, C_ParticlesModule (handles, free-emitter reuse, static emitters from effects.bin
  v1/v2, saved games). Fixed: loaded effects are re-sorted by handle (saved in reverse order, which
  broke FindActive's binary search). i3d.h gained I3D_visual::GetInvMatrix, camera view matrix,
  scene render stamp, I3D_particle_visual::SetBoundVolume (0x74) and I3D_particle_vertex.
- physics objects (`game/physics*.{h,cpp}`, `game/collision.h`): C_PhysicsModule (handles, active/resting
  lists, scene "phobj" frames, net state), base rigid body, simple/falling/can/swinging objects,
  doors/fences/trees (rattle on push, open with edge ray tests, fence break into "plot"/"dira", tree
  swap to "d<model>"), ball and box (never created by the game; ported from the decompilation).
  Fixed: DestroyObject nulled the wrong door entry (inverted test); DeleteAll left dangling doors.
  i3d.h: I3D_frame slots 0x50..0x58 (UpdateBound / GetBound at +0x10), I3D_visual common slots
  (UpdateLocalBound 0x90, GetLocalBound at +0x1A0), I3D_scene AddFrame/RemoveFrame.
- items (`game/item_defs.*`, `game/items.h`, `game/item_containers.cpp`, `game/item_instances.cpp`,
  `game/items_module.*`; 0x7d3650..0x7e0c00 fully ported). Definitions (items.sav records, 10
  component classes, Save/Load streams), item instances (C_Item + ammo / container / medkit / weapon:
  reload rules, firing through the shot manager, shot / reload / bolt sounds, projectile frames,
  saved-game chunks), containers (slot / list / store) and C_ItemsModule: inventory rules
  (MoveItem with all result codes, auto placement, TransferItem swaps between humans,
  IsSwapBlocked, CanPutItemInSlot by type), dropping, uids, actor item save/load (chunk 0x2775,
  item records 0xAD2C), Missions\<m>\items.dat load/save, placements for the temp actors list.
  C_game+0x28 is the mission clock (`GetMissionTime`); +0x7C of C_Actor is the carried weight and
  GetHitPoints() (slot 0x10) what an actor can carry. Human equipment models (human +0xA8), HUD
  panel (C_game +0x5C) and the client "surface marks" (0x78a890..) stay declared in pending.h.
  Original bugs kept and commented: item owner / container chunk ids differ between save and load;
  CreateItem moves the new item twice instead of the displaced one; Done() forgets every other
  instance; the shared weapon sound request leaks the silenced range scale.
- actors (`game/actor.*`, `game/actors_module.*`): C_Actor base with all 56 virtual slots named
  (`types/43_actors.spec` `vslot` lines; `class_bases.json` gives every actor class C_Actor as base so
  their vtables inherit the names), activation/deactivation (initial parent/placement restore),
  saved games (chunk 0x3039), seat interface defaults, hierarchy box corners; C_ActorsModule (player
  slots, active set with deferred changes, delayed removal, spawn points, enemy counts, net dispatch)
  and C_TemporaryActorsList (items, mines, debris, wire-cut fences; save/load chunks 0x456..0x45A,
  join-state and create-mine messages). Item/mine/inventory internals stay declared in pending.h.
  Also ported: C_NetMessage quantised floats/positions/directions (0x73d810..0x73db70).
- shots (`game/shots.*`, 0x448270..0x4559c0, `types/45_shots.spec`, `names/shots.txt`): C_Shot with the
  bullet (segment sweep through the collision world, materials: absorption / penetration cost,
  ricochet once, damage falloff beyond the ideal range, hit zones, version keepers, physics impulses,
  near-miss whiz, tracer and signal-flare visuals, client bullets of remote players), projectile
  (explodes on impact, camera ride-along, fly sound) and thrown item (C_RigidBody + contact callback,
  fuse / impact grenades); C_ShotManager (bullet and shell pools, shells, muzzle lights, bullet holes /
  scorch marks / blood decals, blood drops and pools, explosions, hit sounds, explosion message,
  placeholder save chunks 0x1A0A..0x1A0D), C_HitZones ("humanzones" boxes on the joints, helmet test),
  MeleeAttack (backstab). Learned: S_MaterialDef +0x28 absorption / +0x2C penetration cost; firearm
  +0x3C is the damage falloff per metre (was "unaimed dispersion"); item def +0x3C = shell sound;
  C_Shot +0x6C = fake shot (scripted fake attacks: passes actors, silent to the AI); S_DebrisParams is
  the rigid body's parameter block (+0x20 = initial velocity). Fixed: client bullets are no longer kept
  across missions; a blood pool made without a texture is not dereferenced on reuse; a grenade touching
  a destructible part without a material no longer reads g_materials[-1].
- collision world (`game/collision.{h,cpp}`, `game/collision_ray.cpp`, `game/collision_sweep.cpp`,
  `game/collision_internal.h`; `types/35_collision.spec`, `names/collision.txt`; 0x6ee000..0x7182c0,
  the convex hull tests at 0x7e1360..0x7e1b50 and the wrappers at 0x718700..): the "GifC" level
  collision (frame references, tables of triangles and primitives, 3D cell grid, triangle
  classification for the ray test), the dynamic volume grid, ignored volumes, the box sweep overlap
  test, region queries (volume at a point / in a radius, a frame's primitives, water height range),
  ray tests (brute force over the cells, walking the grid along the ray, lowest layer only, static /
  dynamic / water only), the segment query of bullets, sphere sweeps (CollisionSphereTest: polygons
  tested by face contact, plane crossing, edge cylinders and corner spheres; boxes as their three
  facing faces with shared edge flags) and SlideSphere (up to 10 contact planes kept in the world).
  Learned: flags 0x10000 = water, 0x200000 = skipped by bullets, triangle 0x100 = reversed winding;
  volume shapes 0x43 vertical cylinder, 0x46 scaled box, 0x49 / primitive 0x85 convex hulls (32
  inward planes in a visual's space); internal hit normals point into the surface (capsules
  excepted). Original quirks kept and commented: segment hits on oriented box volumes transform the
  normal as a point; a cylinder volume miss aborts the sphere sweep's volume pass; capsule volumes
  sweep with the world direction. Dead code left to tier 1 (no references): 0x6f3ca0, 0x6f42c0,
  0x6f67d0, 0x6f9520..0x6f9b10, 0x6f9e60..0x6fad50, 0x6fb230..0x6fb830, 0x6fbfb0, 0x6fc250,
  0x6fe110..0x6febd0, 0x703bb0, 0x704b80, 0x705f10, 0x70b590, 0x712dd0, 0x7157b0, 0x716740,
  0x717660..0x7182c0, 0x7189c0.
- humans, phase A (`game/human.{h,cpp}`, `human_combat.cpp`, `human_items.cpp`, `human_senses.cpp`,

  4 tracks (0 body animation, 1 carry, 2 activity, 3 the animation player's specials) on pairs of

  motion velocity (two sets, the heavier wins, limited to maxSpeed) plus AddMove / MoveTo /
  AddMove2 / Slide (80 ms steps) give the move; note keys of the animations come through the
  "a" frame's callback (I3D_frame::SetCallback, slot 0x28; I3DCALLBACK_ANIM_NOTE 0x1000) as
  track events (E_AnimEvent, now in human_movement.h), holds ('p'), steps ('s', 180 / 1.4 ms
  apart) and sounds ('!' muzzle light, '$' file, '@' action chapter). Saved as chunk 0x378.
  C_HumanWork (`human_work.cpp`, 0x77e1e0..0x780c70; layout in `human_body.h`): S_MoveRequest
  (its first 0xA0 bytes) now has its fields: killer / working frame / gesture pointers (cleared by
  the load), look-at and facing directions, the `changed` bits (WORK_REQUEST_*), animation,
  activity, stance (+0x4C; was "Unknown77f500"), gait (+0x50, the sub-variant of the moving
  animations; was "SetStance"), carry mode, action, hit flags (zone bits + the side the hit came
  from) and position / force, gesture flags / argument. Requests are refused (m_refused) while
  the blocking masks (+0xC0 / +0xD0 / +0xD4 / +0xD8) have their bit, unless forced (+0xB0);
  Update turns them into the movement's next animation state (special moves, carry animations
  from the carry mode and the actions, hit reactions 0x1E..0x33) and copies the record to the
  movement. Saved as chunk 0x309.
  C_HumanAnimPlayer (`human_anim_player.cpp`, 0x777d10..0x77e1e0): each update takes the
  movement's next animation state (the work's requests) and starts it on the mixer's tracks 0..2
  from the animation tables (body: block 1 anim*3+stance, block 2 the moving ids >= 190 with 6
  gaits; carry: per carry mode, id*3+variant; activities), queueing it while a blend runs. The
  states of an update live in statics shared by all humans (kept). Starting a single-stage
  animation sets what follows it (m_pendingAnim / Carry / Activity), after how long (its length
  less the follower's blend time, / 1.4) and which work requests are blocked meanwhile (ORed into
  C_HumanWork +0xD0); it also does the climbs over obstacles (heights 0.244..1.642, head dummy
  "HD" reset, trail spheres kept), the vehicle doors (facing the "klika" handle), the slides, lying
  down / getting up (stance timers), picking up bodies, drawing / putting away the hands item
  (sounds, HUD inventory events, remote actions 0xD..0xF for the prone ones) and the activities'
  props (copies of the activity props in equipment place 7). PlayAnimation (scripts) plays a
  cached file on track 3. Saved as chunk 0x1BC.
  C_HumanIdle (`human_idle.cpp`, 0x750940..0x758050): Update does the standing fidgets of the
  player's soldiers (0x2F..0x35 / 0x39..0x3D / 0x41 every 15.5..25.5 s), the swimming bob (the
  movement's +0x234 is a float phase, now m_swimBobPhase, that the locomotion feeds through a sine)
  and the breath vapour of cold missions (particles 0x11 / 0x4C at the head, outdoors only).
  "PlayGesture" was the human's interaction handler (debug string "Akce!!!", Czech for "action"):
  the chain C_Human / S_HumanBody / C_HumanWork / C_HumanMovement / C_HumanIdle ::PlayGesture is now
  Interact(C_Actor *target, uint32_t interaction, int arg), BODY_COMMAND_INTERACT,
  WORK_REQUEST_INTERACT and S_MoveRequest::interactTarget / interaction / interactArg. The codes
  (E_Interaction in human_movement.h) are doors, entering / leaving the seats of cars, submarines,
  static weapons and Ju 88s (from the controllers' actor-type switch), sitting on chairs and getting
  up, healing, manipulating, tying up / being tied up, cutting with the wire cutters, backstabbing /
  being backstabbed; INTERACT_FLAG_BODY (0x400) makes the body take part. A simulated human acts at
  once; the others replay the steps queued in m_remoteActions (stance, animation, putting the hands
  item away). UpdateInteraction (was Unknown755960) runs the seat state machine (movement +0xA34
  m_interaction, +0xA38 m_interactionTime, +0xA44 m_seatMode: the seat flags SEAT_FLAG_* (actor.h),
  then 1..8; +0xA48 m_seatStance). The "idle" animation table is the seat-weapon gunner poses
  (C_SeatWeaponAnimTable / g_seatWeaponAnimTable: mg42 / w97 per stance, jeep 1919, flak seat).
  C_HumanSpecialMoves (`human_special_moves.cpp`, 0x74a020..0x750940): what the body may do where
  it is, tested with its stance spheres against the collision world: CanTurn (turning in place
  while swimming / leaning / lying), AdjustLean (how far the leans 0xA2..0xA5 go before the head
  touches: the animation player's m_unknownA0 hold fraction), TryClimbOver (the obstacle's
  height picks the climbing animation 0x0A..0x0D, 0x0E from a ladder; trail spheres over it;
  something overhead goes to the contact code, ProcessMoveContacts 0x788ca0, ported with the
  locomotion), the ladders (UseLadder, EnterLadderFromTop / CanEnterLadderFromBottom; the C_Ladder
  top +0xA0, bottom +0xB0, facing +0xD0, climbers vector +0x164 through pending.h accessors),
  UpdateWater (the locomotion's +0x108 m_waterDepth surface-to-ground and +0x10C m_waterHeight
  feet-to-surface; true: swim), HasRoomForStance (lying down / standing up / getting up, the body
  shifted in steps), and Die: the death animation from the hit's side and kind (drowning 0x9A,
  0x74..0x76, backstabs 0x98 / 0x99, seats 0x92..0x97 / 0x72, falls 0x77..0x8F with room checked
  by HasRoomToFall; a clock-seeded counter g_deathSeed picks the variants), then the dead body's
  state (volumes, sounds, the carried body dropped, the weapon pose's reaction).
  C_HumanLocomotion (`human_locomotion.cpp`, 0x763950..0x7724d0, the contact helpers 0x788a60..
  0x78a020; the body sweeps C_CollisionWorld::MoveSpheres / MoveSpheresStepping / MoveSpheresLow
  0x71ba20..0x71cba0 in `collision_body.cpp`, S_MoveContacts now in collision.h with its fields):
  Update picks the move of the body's state. On the ground (MoveOnGround): the mixer's move, a
  push off edges and the fall swept with the stance spheres (walking lifts them 0.3 m to step up,
  lying / swimming 0.1 m), the slowdown on slopes and the slide factor of steep ground (movement
  +0x758, a float: m_slideFactor); the AI's cheap mode (MOVE_FLAG_GROUND_RAY 0x2: a ray down) and
  path height (MOVE_FLAG_FOLLOW_PATH 0x4). Climbing 8..0xE follows the head dummy; jumps; ladders
  (off at the top through TryClimbOver); swimming (bob, pitch); dying / dead bodies (aligned to
  the ground, the blood pool position +0x9F0 / +0xA00, slides off edges). Falls speed up 3.27 m/s
  per 85 ms; fall damage 240 per m over 3 m (60 over 6 m into water; deep water saves; network
  games only with the session's fallDamage, S_MultiplayerSettings +0x258); the landing animations
  0x14..0x16 by the height, the free fall 0x11 over 5 m. +0xA90 is m_parachuting: a soldier of the
  player's squad starting over 20 m up comes down at 5 m/s without fall damage. Turning: the turn
  in place animations 0x2B / 0x2C or the steady turn (0.006 / 0.008 rad/ms); UpdateVelocity sets
  the root motion speed from g_moveSpeeds[13][18] (0x816878; the visual's two copies replaced);
  ChangeStance (the stance change animations 0x1A..0x21 after HasRoomForStance); the collision
  volumes and dummies follow the logical position (the frame put there and back); UpdateDetail
  sets the detail bits by the size on the screen (MOVE_FLAG_10..1000 renamed LARGE, FULL_DETAIL,
  SMALL, TINY, HIDDEN, NOT_DRAWN, IN_RANGE, + OUT_OF_RANGE 0x2000); the swimming sound (heard by
  g_cameraUnderWater, 0x98a6ac). ProcessMoveContacts: physics objects pushed, humans told (the
  side returned), a ladder walked into used, a closed door opened, trains / barrels told. Movement
  fields named: +0x228 m_stillTime, +0x761 m_onDynamicGround, +0x762 m_onGround.
  Not ported (no callers): 0x76d350, 0x788b60, 0x789220, 0x7893a0 and the earlier sweep variants
  0x7189c0 / 0x7194a0 / 0x71a080 / 0x71af10.
  The weapon pose: see below.
  Fixed: UpdateStanceVolumes overran the 4 stance spheres with two stances at once and indexed them
  with -10000 when a dummy was missing; the empty sound group 16 crashed the bank; the footprint
  counters were indexed by net id without a check; trail spheres loaded beyond 4; the cloth sound
  roll divided by a stealth stat of 0; Init / Reset of the visual read the anim table of an unknown
  group; the morph's dead face read the morph visual of a model without one; the mixer's track
  events overflowed their 5 slots, the note sound copy its 256 bytes, '!' notes read past short
  text, HoldTrack / ReleaseTrack on a free track wrote the rate of the stage before the pair, a
  saved stage 8 / an animation file no longer found crashed LoadGame, the note hook called a
  human without movement. The anim player read near null with no animation table (group not
  1 / 2), called through null for a door without a vehicle, a picked-up frame without an actor and
  a holstered item without a component, indexed past the carry table's end with variant 3 / 4 of
  the last id, and wrote the activity's hide event past the track's 5 events. Interact read the
  seat to move a seat's occupant to from an uninitialised stack slot when the human did not change
  seats (fixed: the occupant gets out). PushOutOfWalls (not called) left the body's collision
  ignored after a hit (fixed). RelinkModel had lost its second argument (the previous model whose
  stages the mixer takes over). C_Human::ControllerUnknownD0 (0x5a5c90) had lost its argument
  too: it is the controller's SetMoveFlags (slot 0xD0): the work tells it the stance and pace,
  the animation player the direction of the animation (MOVE_FORWARD / BACK / LEFT / RIGHT,
  leaning), the movement the medium (ladder, climbing, sliding, under water; the port had
  these flags as "computed but not used"). The other controller wrappers that popped arguments
  their declarations lacked (found by comparing every names/human.txt prototype with the RET n
  of the code, thunks followed into the controller slot) are fixed and named the same way:
  slots 0x34 / 0x38 TestSightOfMeleeTracker / TestSightOfHuman (target, int *status, float *visibility:
  the sight module's view test, 100 inside the human's field of view within sight range; were
  ControllerUnknown34 with 2 of 3 arguments / ControllerUnknown38 with none), 0x58
  AdjustToPathHeight(S_vector *pos) (the locomotion's next position put on the AI's path),
  0x90 SetUnconscious() (= OnKnockedOut; C_Human::SetUnconscious(C_Human *by) is the client
  side of KnockOut, `by` unused), 0xF8 SendRemoteRequest(int) (network message 0xF ->
  C_HumanMovement::OnRemoteAction; the ported TryClimbOver / EnterLadderFromTop now send the
  E_RemoteRequest of the climb, they sent nothing), C_Human::ScriptedUnknown58e880(int, int) /
  ScriptedUnknown590750(int) (unreferenced). Overrides named: C_AIController 0x56fc80 / 0x570280
  / 0x57fbc0 (+ IsHumanWithin 0x57fe40), C_PlayerController::TestSightOfHuman 0x542320. Quirk
  kept: the local player's TestSightOfMeleeTracker leaves `visibility` unset (the sight module then
  compares its uninitialised local). Quirks kept: the mixer's Init resets 7 of the 8 stages; the 'k' /
  'u' notes add to track 0 at track 1's event count; NotifyFrames moves ids[count] (one past the
  last) into a found id's place; the extra move (m_extraMove) loses its last part and its times
  are never initialised (so the visual's "standing" flag, which waits for them, stays clear);
  GetBlendProgress clears track 0's blend progress; QueueActivity does nothing. The work's
  SetFacingDir / SetViewDir / ApplyHit copied the 4 bytes after their vectors into the w slots
  (fixed: left). Work quirks kept: SetFacingDir marks its change whatever `on` says;
  SetUnknown5C cannot be forced; SetStanceTimer does not keep its third argument (so the timer's
  block mask is only ever loaded); Play counts its requests without a use. Anim player quirks kept: a
  human of another group keeps the previous human's animation table; the moving animations'
  table blend time is not scaled by the queue speed; starting an activity resets track 1's rate;
  carry variants 3 / 4 read the next id's slots; the standing animations clear the controller's
  move flags twice; PlayAnimation builds an error message it never shows. Idle / interaction quirks
  kept: the vapour skips gaits 1, 2 and 5; a new interaction drops the remote steps only while the
  movement's clock is 0; entering a seat sets the controller's move flags to GetMoveMode() & 7
  (always 0); taking a dead occupant out looks the "q" dummy up in the wrong model and refreshes
  the wrong model in the scene; INTERACT_SIT sets the seat mode before it may refuse; the end of
  being tied up leaves the work blocked; the hit volumes get 0 whether the seat hides the occupant
  or not. SpecialMoves quirks kept: side hits become ahead / behind at random and HasRoomToFall
  always fails for the sides, so the side deaths (0x81 / 0x82 / 0x8B / 0x8C) never play; the
  surface angle of HasRoomToFall carries over between spheres; EnterLadderFromTop restores a
  collision it has not removed; the trail spheres get an uninitialised w (0 here); getting up
  takes 100 / 1.4 ms either way. Locomotion quirks kept: MoveDying ends the states 2..6 (most
  deaths) at once, so the dead-body alignment and slides run only for 7..9 (their tests of 2..6
  never hold); the dead body's fall is passed no time (it never speeds up); a falling animation
  touching something beeps (MessageBeep); the local player's frame drift along x is not seen by
  UpdateCollisionVolumes; the smaller fall spheres compare the ground's height, not the height
  above it, with 2 m; Land ends the parachute only on landings over 9.81 m/s (never reached on
  it); MoveSpheresStepping sets the end only after a contact when nothing is below; the cheap
  mode's ground ray ORs 0x800000 into the contacts instead of its filter; UpdateTurn's instant
  turn adds the time to the turn counter (+0x950); a muted swimming sound is renewed at full
  volume once mid fade in. Fixed: UpdateVelocity passed an uninitialised velocity for the ladder
  animations while held and an uninitialised direction without a ladder. Quirks kept:
  OnDropBody refreshes the carrier's own hit volumes typed as a dead body's; OnFrozen drops the hit
  volumes' material; the human fading shows its footprints when fading out and hides them when
  fading in; chunk 0x26 saves a float as int and loads the int's bits back; loaded footprints are
  less deep (0.6) and not counted for their humans; ProcessRemoteActions leaves the work forced
  when it drops a climb; the visual's pick-up event reads the A2C bit after resetting it (always
  event 4) and releases the 0x5A object once more than the 0x4F one; the ladder step event without a
  ladder stops the event handling; the dead face is only shown when the camera is not near; unknown
  morph states carry the previous channel's state index over. C_HumanController::Unknown5C takes (float *intensity, float *reference).
- human weapon pose (`game/human_weapon_pose.{h,cpp}`, `human_pose_ik.cpp`, `human_pose_body.cpp`,
  `human_pose_seats.cpp`, `human_pose_hold.cpp`, `human_pose_weapon.cpp`, `human_pose_ragdoll.cpp`,
  `human_pose_physics.cpp`, `human_pose_dead.cpp`, `human_pose_internal.h`; 0x79bd70..0x7ce4d0;
  types in `47_movement.spec`, names in `names/weapon_pose.txt`, 134 entries, which override movement.txt /
  human.txt): C_HumanWeaponPose (0x6950, movement +0xDC0) poses the 23 bones (g_poseBoneNames "u".."a",
  0x816d18) over the animation: the chains' IK (two-bone solve, joint limits by direction components in
  the parent's axes, stiffness tables), holding the weapon standing / crouching / prone / seated (pistol
  and rifle grips on the LW / RW / BUTT dummies, LWC / RWC in cars, LWS / RWS on seats), raising /
  lowering / pausing, the support hand, recoil, activities, head looks and glances, steering, static
  weapons, the prone body fit, saved games (chunk 0xDEF0, 0xDEFEDEFE markers; anim sets by file name).
  Death: kinds 0 FALL, 2 SEATED, 3 HELD (7 held points swept to the ground), 4 RAGDOLL; the death
  animation (speed x1.5), the dead body's slide (gravity 45, ground fit clamped to 10 degrees), hit
  impulses, blood pools, picking a body up. The ragdoll is a Verlet simulation of 11 bodies (capsules
  carrying bones) and 16 points: 3..6 substeps by the movement detail, collisions on odd substeps and
  the last, corrections averaged per point by body mass, 8 pushes and 2 holds, contact frames (saved by
  name), settling, 100 ms steps blended for display, impact / drop sounds (chapter 0 group 0x2C,
  chapter 1 group 0x37). The old header's getters were 4 bytes off (+0x62E0 is the weapon); the spec
  offsets are absolute. Quirks kept: SetLook's end of a look keeps the old flags (no return); LoadGame
  cannot restore chain limits 3..5 (unreachable); Update / ApplyChain / the prone fit SetPos joints
  with world coordinates; AddHumanModel gets the model's z and q swapped; AddRagdollHold writes the
  hold time into the push slot; the settle rate divides and squares its static twice; the segment test
  projects with an unnormalised axis; KeepCamerasOutOfWalls sets the world hit as the dummies' local
  position; the dead pose tests 0x10 ("on vehicle", the static weapon bit). Fixed: NewRagdollBody leaked
  itself on a full parent; StartRagdoll used a dropped part without a null check; UpdateDead's FPV camera
  used a missing head point; SetViewDir left w uninitialised; the slide / limit matrices' stale w values
  are zeroed. C_HumanStains (0x796700..0x79b0a0: stain manager 0x98adf0, mesh graph, texture compositing)
  is declared in `game/human_stains.h` and called, not ported. Not ported (no callers): 0x79bf00,
  0x79bf10, 0x7a2d60, 0x7a33c0, 0x7a3760, 0x7a4a90, 0x7a4c50, 0x7b35a0, 0x7b7e10, 0x7b7ea0, 0x7c5bb0,
  0x7c67e0, 0x7c6a70, 0x7cae80, 0x7cbe80, 0x7cfa10, the bone turns 0x7baa40 / 0x7bfc10; the debug draw 0x79b0a0 / 0x79bbc0; 0x7ce930 is the
  AI's path smoothing (caller 0x58cce0).
- game core (`game/game.{h,cpp}`, `game_rounds.cpp`, `game_save.cpp`, `game_state.h`, `game_internal.h`;
  0x006987d0..0x0069f3c0 except GetCommandProcessor 0x0069f520; the scene loader C_game::LoadMission 0x006caad0
  is declared, not ported; types in `10_core.spec`, names in `names/game.txt`): C_game (0xEC) derives from
  C_GameState (vtable 0x008158ac, 13 slots: Start / Stop return bool, HandleInput(held, pressed), Tick (the
  server's 100 ms), Update (frames), Render, GetTypeId (the game: 1), the state accessors; now in
  `game_state.h` with the state values). Mission start / stop (the squad spawns at the spawn points, the
  scene's static lights are off for the mission, the HUD objects' Init / Done, the material and vehicle
  tables), restart (rounds keep the actors), saved games (chunk 0x29A; 9000 the clock and switches; 3 saves
  at difficulty 3, none at 4, none in the very first mission), input (quick save / load, the HUD toggle, the
  menu, skipping a cutscene), pause, music / alarms, the light level of a point (point / spot / directional /
  ambient lights, occlusion rays). Multiplayer: warmup (from the first tick; players frozen and spectating;
  `warmup` s), the round's end per game type every second (deathmatch: point / time limit; occupation: the
  zones held and the team points; objectives: attackers (the first objective's team) against defenders, a
  team with players but none alive loses, elimination bonus 2, the defenders get the points of the
  objectives they kept, a VIP among the defenders (OBJECTIVE_TYPE_VIP, special uniform); co-op: lives per
  player or shared), 4 s result, 5 s intermission, `roundCount` rounds then the next map, respawn waves,
  respawn requests (zones, co-op lives), players joining / spawning / leaving, the spectator camera. Names
  from the data: S_MultiplayerSettings fields are the server config's keys (0x006ad200: pointlimit,
  roundlimit, roundcount, warmup, respawntime, allowrespawn, friendlyfire, autoteambalance, ... teamlives;
  `teamGame` is `gameType`, E_MpGameType 0 deathmatch, 1 occupation, 2 objectives, 3 cooperative; +0x170 is
  `dedicated`, was g_clientWorldReady); 0x0086933c is the script module's cutscene flag (was g_fadeEnabled;
  app.cpp draws the letterbox with it); S_SoldierState +0x04 spawnZone, +0x09 vip, +0x44 deployed;
  S_NetPlayerState connection / stats / left / livesUsed; C_Team::SetWatchedHuman (was SetViewMode),
  GetSelectedMember; C_PlayerController::HandleInput / IsVip; app commands QUICK_SAVE / QUICK_LOAD / SAVE /
  LOAD. Quirks kept: the mission's completed / failed message follows the status of the objective decided
  last; saves are counted at difficulty 3 even when nothing is saved; a short chunk 9000 loads the values of
  the last save / load (a static buffer); LoadGame stays inside a chunk of another id; OnWarmupFinished
  opens a second in-game menu over the game and drops it; EndMission's argument (100) is not used; the
  first objective is read without a check; the round check's tail timer is dead code; a spawn's w and the
  occupation spawn direction's w are stack garbage (0 here). Dropped: the copy protection made objectives
  fail critically now and then. Fixed: AddAnimatedModel took a reference also for models it did not keep;
  RestartMission freed the pending spawns without their soldier state and stats; OnRespawnRequest leaked
  them when the co-op lives were used up; FailMission read the objectives of a missing mission. Ghidra: the
  C_GameState / C_game layouts, S_MissionObjective, C_MissionDef (the campaign node class is not
  identified), S_PendingSpawn, S_MultiplayerSettings, S_NetPlayerState, opaque C_Interface / C_HudPanel /
  C_QuickMessages / C_Checkpoints / C_GameApp / C_NetGame / C_Campaign / C_LoaderScreen / C_StainManager /
  C_MpMap; the auto classes 0x00815750 / 0x0081659c are C_GameMenu / C_CommandProcessor (class_names.json,
  rename_auto_structs, apply_members); I3D_scene slot 0 is Release (the scene is a frame), 0x74
  SetRenderCallback, 0x78 Render, 0xF8 Close.
