  holds the walking network, then the road network: C_Checkpoints (C_game +0x54, 0x78 bytes) derives
  from the walking C_CheckpointNetwork (0x3C), GetRoadNetwork() = this + 0x3C.
  * data: LoadCheckpointNetwork (0x00523c00, stdcall(chunk, network)) reads headers of versions
    0x7ED00101 (5 ints), ..02 (6), ..03-..09 (7) and returns the version when the data parses to its
    full size, else 0; C_CheckpointNetwork::Load only takes version 9 (header: version, size, point
    count, link slots, name / frame / actor counts). S_Checkpoint (0x24): pos; cell = GetCell(x)
    (10 m columns; the points are sorted by cell, then z); preference (links cost (a + b)/1024 less);
    radius (1/16 m); flags (link count 0x3F, 0x40 a spare slot for joins, 0x80 joined, 0x100 no stop,
    0x200 / 0x2000 set by the cars, 0x400 links in the shared storage; 0x800 / 0x4000 / 0x8000 in the
    data, unknown); type (1 doors, 3 ladders by their actors' frames; 15 for FindNearestType15); the
    cover masks of stances 1 / 2 / 4 by compass direction (GetDirectionBit, 8 bits); four values n/254
    (GetValuePair); the links {target, cost in 1/256 m}. Tables: named points (16 characters), points
    tied to frames (none in the shipped data) and to actors (doors' sides etc., sorted by point).
  * spatial lookups scan cell columns outward: FindNearest (less the points' radius), FindNearestPoint
    (plain), FindNearestType15 (xz plane; the AI's fallback), MarkWithin, FindCoverWithin.
  * reachability (FloodFill: link costs summed up to a radius; a point keeps the first way found):
    GetNeighbours / GetReachable / GetReachableCopy / GetNeighbourCover / GetReachableCover,
    GetReachableDepthFirst, GetReachableType12, FindNearestFree; PickCoverPoint (flags 0x1 from self,
    0x4 farthest, 0x700 stance covered, 0x6000 stance not covered, 0x40000000 / 0x80000000 removals);
    GetPathLength.
  * path searches: A* with a Manhattan estimate in 1/256 m and node pools (statics of the original).
    They stop when a link first reaches the goal, not when it leaves the open list (2726 of 4305
    test paths were longer than the shortest). FindPath (exclude mask & 0xFB40), FindMeleeTrackerPath (no
    closed doors, links 3-3 (ladders), 4-4, 6-5), FindEscapePath (from threat positions to a point
    safeDist from all, never within minDist).
  * joins (the scripts' CheckpointJoin / Separate) use the points' spare slots. Saved games: chunk
    0x81 holds the walking network's joins (0x4C5, pairs a | b << 16), 0x82 and 0x84 are written
    empty (the road network's joins are not saved); ResetState undoes them.
  Fixed: FindCoverWithin never freed its marks; the searches' static arrays held 10000 points (the
  biggest shipped network has 7150); a join chunk with 1-3 bytes left looped forever; FindByPrefix's
  strstr read past 16-character names; LoadCheckpointNetwork overran its buffer for a data size
  smaller than the header.
  Quirks kept: GetCell puts -10 into cell -2; FindFirst returns 0, not `lo`; Clear keeps the joins;
  JoinPoints keeps a half join; FindNearestFree returns 0 without points; MarkWithin marks every point
  for radius 0. The game's float math is single precision (the data's cells only match float
  products), so the harness compiles with SSE math. C_HumanController::GetCurrentCheckpoint (+0xA4) is
  an S_Checkpoint *, not an index (the scripts' MoveRandomRadius passed it as one).
  Not ported (no callers): a Dijkstra search 0x0051ec70 / 0x0051eb80 with its node pool (0x0051ea60..
  0x0051eb00, 0x00523200, 0x00523400 / 0x00523420 / 0x00523700) whose only caller is a debug
  comparison with FindPath (0x005211c0); FindNameEntry 0x0051e150 and the table searches 0x0051e2c0 /
  0x0051e450 / 0x0051e5e0; CollectReachableWithCosts 0x0051ff20 / 0x00520110; S_Checkpoint::ClearLinks
  0x005245e0; LoadCheckpointTestFile 0x00523e40 (a debug loader of "ctest.bin").
  Test harness: `_re/harness/checkpoints/build.bat` loads the 73 missions' check2.bin (both networks
  to the end of the file; all 839888 stored link costs match GetLinkCost) and checks the lookups,
  searches, joins and cover picks against brute-force references.
  Tier 1 after the port: 8535 functions, 1032 errors in 153/615 files, 380 stubs (8629 / 1034 / 155 /
  388 before). The spec import left a stray empty `/wchar_t` struct in Ghidra (nothing referenced it;
  renamed to zz_junk_wchar_t so tier 1 cannot emit `struct wchar_t`).
- controllers, phase 1 (2026-10-18; the network messages `game/controller_messages.*` 0x005b0e20..
  0x005b30d0 with Write/ReadControlState 0x005ac5f0 / 0x005ac660; the base class C_HumanController
  `game/human_controller.*` 0x00582ff0..0x00589290 and 0x0058b6c0..0x0058c510, with the base-vtable
  defaults 0x00524d30.. / 0x0056f090..; names in `names/human.txt` (controller block) and
  `names/controller.txt` (the world-object, HUD and replication callees), types in `46_human.spec`).
  * messages: a 5-bit type, the queue ended by type 24 (E_ControllerMessage); 25 structs with their
    Read / Write. The original builds S_MsgTransferItem with type 7 (CTRL_MSG_MOVE_ITEM) everywhere
    but its Write sends 8, so the type field is harmless.
  * C_HumanController (vtable 0x00810fb8, 67 slots, 0xC0 bytes): the full member layout (events,
    stance at Attach, walk speed, the asked pace +0x5C, the interaction +0x60..+0x6C, the controls'
    axes / buttons +0x74 / +0x78, the human's C_NetObject +0x7C, the message queues to the server /
    the clients +0x80 / +0x90, the checkpoints +0xA0 / +0xA4, alarm +0xB5). Renamed slots:
    ControlVehicle (0x00), RequestAction (0x08: action, pos, dir, throw strength, the reload's
    magazine uid and rounds), EndInteraction (0x0C), Heal (0x10), UseMedkit (0x14),
    RequestLeaveVehicle (0x44), RequestPickUpItem (0x6C), RequestTakeFromBox (0x7C), ExecuteCommand
    (0xDC), UseActor (0xE0), LeaveVehicle (0xE4). E_HumanCommandType lists the 20 commands of category
    2 (doors, mines, explosives, lockpicking, healing, wire cutting, tying up, car charges, ladders,
    climbing, documents, self-healing, backstabs) with their durations; E_ControllerAction the
    RequestAction actions.
  * the controls (player and remote controllers): ApplyMoveControls (walk / lean animations by the
    direction change, the walk vector times the speed, the gait, crouch / lie toggles, the dive for 10
    stamina, BUTTON_CLIMB = command 0x1F), DecodeControls (a remote human's velocity and control bits
    back into controls), ApplyWeaponControls (seat weapons; in a vehicle only the driver has no hand
    weapon; the belt grenade; grenades in the hands; no automatic fire for behaviours 2 / 4 / 6; reload
    (enemies keep the last magazine); raise; empty = reload; fire from the shot dummy after the
    component's attack delay, C_WeaponItem +0x58 now m_attackTime), UseItem (the local player aims
    with the camera, grenades 0.3 upward).
  * also: speech (Say / SayNow: a server sends it only when a player in play is within twice the
    code's variant; the AI noise call does nothing on a dedicated server, as in the original), stamina
    (an integer division makes the endurance term 0 or 1), healing (the first aid skill's share of
    full health), saved games (chunks 0x457 state, 0x458 interaction, 0x459 speech).
  Fixed: the destructor freed the vectors but not the queued messages; null hands container / item
  (UseItem fired through a null item; laying a mine on the server read a missing item's definition);
  the shot dummy was read past the end of non-weapon items; saving during a mine-laying read the
  missing actor; a missing door / document place; the car charge's uninitialised point when the ray
  misses; SayNow without a speech set; the climb's net property unchecked.
  Quirks kept: LeaveVehicle without a vehicle or body ends the interaction unless it is 0 (so also
  with none going on); Say returns the low byte of the sample's length; SayNow passes the speech code
  to the sound module's SetFlashLight; DecodeControls presses crouch with 2.0.
  Found on the way: C_Human::FindContainerOfBehaviour returns the item (UseMedkit uses it); the
  C_Human wrappers 0x005a2cf0 / 0x005a2d20 pass an actor id (now RequestTakeFromBox(box, index) /
  RequestPickUpItem(itemActor)); C_Actor slot 0xC0 is ApplySeatControls(seat, axes, buttons);
  ret_audit found C_Car::ApplyControls(axes, buttons), C_PhotoPlace::TakePhoto(human),
  C_ExplosivePlace::Disarm(human) (the decompile showed them without the last argument).
  Not ported here: the AI task pools in the range (0x00589310..0x0058b660, phase 2 with
  C_AIController); 0x0058c320 (empty, called by the player controller).
  Tier 1 after the port: 8385 functions, 1029 errors in 153/613 files, 381 stubs (8535 / 1032 / 153 /
  380 before).
- controllers, phase 2: the AI base C_AIController (2026-10-18; `game/ai_controller.cpp` life cycle,
  network side, saved games; `ai_senses.cpp` sight tests, targets, the perception handlers;
  `ai_combat.cpp` the fire order, aim, grenades, looking, stance; `ai_movement.cpp` driving and
  walking; `ai_parts.{h,cpp}` the parts and pools; shared controller helpers moved to
  `controller_internal.h`. 0x0056ee90..0x00582030, the parts 0x0055d1e0..0x00563c70 and 0x00576ac0..
  0x00576d00, the tasks' saved games 0x0054fc40..0x00551690, the pools 0x00582030..0x00582df0 with
  their templates 0x00589310..0x0058b660; names in `names/ai.txt`, types in `46_human.spec`).
  * parts: S_AIFacing (+0x124: the body's facing and the view within a cone of 0.7, 0.94 lying,
    locks, looking around), S_AIFireOrder (+0x128), S_AIMoveOrder (+0x12C: the destination and the
    state of following the route; flags named), C_AIDriver (+0x130: the road path, a Hermite curve
    through the road points moved within their radius), S_AIAlertState (+0x134), S_AIFire (+0xC0),
    S_AITask types 1..12 (+0xF0), S_AIWaypoint (0x30: the point's position, own position, index,
    flags & 0xFB40, type, radius in m). Pools at 0x008ab158 as C_ObjectPool.
  * movement: UpdateMove (E_MoveResult) plans a route over the walking network (from the ladder's
    far end when on one; enemies avoid CHECKPOINT_FLAG_NO_STOP points; swimming without a path goes
    straight; fleeing uses FindEscapePath against the enemies seen), cuts its corners within the
    points' radius, raises type 15 runs (swimming, inferred) to at least 1 m over their points, then
    follows it: doors (face the waypoint, lower the weapon, command 0x1B, step back while it swings
    toward the human), ladders (command 0x1E; enemies wait while someone else is on it), climbs
    (command 0x1F, once a second in network games; types 4/4, 6/5, 5/5 over 0.3 m; 5/6/5 within
    0.5..3 m: the dive button), cars standing on points (step off to the nearest free point, wait
    5..10 s), type 15 runs step round the humans in the way (detour waypoints 0.9 m beside them
    where there is ground). Reaching a waypoint: 0.1 m, or up to 0.5 m after steps that brought it
    no nearer, or walking past it; 30 such steps end the move. Walking: the body turns toward the
    waypoint; with the view locked it walks forward / back / sideways / diagonally with hysteresis
    (12.5..77.5 degrees by the current direction); the pace from the move order, held back at
    ladders, doors and points of flag 0x800. Stepping aside (C_HumanAIMemory::unknown00, now a
    float[3]) while no route is followed. Unknown5C interpolates the points' light values along the
    segment walked; AdjustToPathHeight their heights.
  * driving: the car steers to the curve point ahead (a third further along than the car will be in
    4 frames, never back), blending to the tangent within 3 m; speed = the car's +0x780 times the
    pace's factor (the script's speed for the scripted AI), less in turns and over the last 4 m; 5 s
    without getting nearer skips a segment, 3 s standing backs up; the scripted AI's script goes on
    at its label on arrival.
  * network: the AI human's C_NetObject (UpdateNetwork: position / direction tracks, control bits,
    the dive), RequestAction / Freeze, OnMessage (clients) / OnMessage2 (the server), FlushMessages
    (actor messages 0x00475530 / 0x00475570 / 0x004755b0, now ported in actors_module.cpp);
    EncodeControlBits 0x005ac550 (the counterpart of DecodeControls) ported in human_controller.cpp.
  Fixed: the destructor left the waypoints and the task out of their pools; Attach / RouteToCheckpoint
  / NeedsNewRoute used a missing walking network; RequestAction and the attack / ready times read
  weapon fields of any item; OnHit without a head container; UpdateFireOrder without a weapon; the
  saved game's waypoint loop and a null task; the driver read past a path of one point (PlanPath and
  the driving); UseLadder made the clients' message on a client too and leaked it; the first
  waypoint of an empty route (IsOnPlainPoints, SmoothRouteTo); a type 1 point's actor taken for a
  door unchecked; the nearby actors taken for humans (gone ones and other kinds too); the scripts'
  HUMAN_Drive / DriveNearPlayer stopped the car through any controller (AsAIController now).
  Quirks kept: SetPos keeps the flee flag; ThrowGrenade zeroes throwPos.y; NearerPoint compares the
  squared distances less the (unsquared) radii; FixRouteHeights interpolates each waypoint from the
  one before; the AI's IssueCommand override is bypassed by its own door / ladder / climb commands;
  the driver's pace 0x1000 and 0x8000 both drive at a quarter.
  Found on the way: C_Door +0x234 is the lock (GetState 4) and +0x224 the motion (0 closed, 1 open,
  2 / 3 moving); C_Car +0x4A0 the velocity, 0x004d9280 an input setter; S_AIFacing 0x0055d1f0
  LookToward / 0x0055d280 SetLookAround (called by the derived controllers); m_unknown114 / 118 start
  at -1; C_AIController's +0x11C is the scripts' IgnoreCircumvention flag; C_Human::GetAIPathPoints
  (0x005a85d0, was AIUnknown57a390).
  Not ported (no callers; tier 1): FaceTowards 0x00570ac0, GetWeaponPos 0x005715f0, GetHitChance
  0x00571df0, GetTargetDistance 0x00573310 / 0x00575350, GetNearestEnemy 0x00573500, CanSeeHeadOf
  0x00575150, GetViewDotTo 0x00575410, SetRoutePoints 0x0057af70 with ClearRoute 0x0057f7d0,
  SkipPassedWaypoint 0x0057b1b0, IsNearSelectedSoldier 0x0057ee30, IsPosNearSegment 0x00580120,
  DrawRouteDebug 0x00580800, FindActorNear 0x00581440, the driver's route planner 0x0055e2f0 /
  0x0055e6a0 with its helpers; SegmentDistance2 0x00581210 and S_Vector4Array::Add 0x00581f20 stay
  for their other callers (the port has a local copy of the first); the squad's pools (body
  commands, orders 0x00582550..0x00582640) come with phase 3.
  Tier 1 after the port (all decompiles exported again): 8080 functions, 1042 errors in 154/611
  files, 397 stubs (8385 / 1029 / 153 / 381 before). The failures around the AI are in the derived
  controllers' files (phase 3): locals the decompiler types S_AIWaypoint * / C_Checkpoints * but
  reuses as floats (the bitcast patch breaks on the multi-line expressions), the enemy / type 8
  vtables' old slot names in Ghidra (vf3f, Unknown00), and the thunks 0x0056a8a0 / 0x0056a8c0
  named after their targets (duplicate definitions). raw/functions.json predates GetPathPoints
  0x0057a390 (created in Ghidra later): export it with `export_decomp.py --only`.
- controllers, phase 3: the squad controller C_SquadController (2026-10-18; the original's
  C_PlayerAiBrain, its action _S_CURRENTACTIONDESC and its orders "map commands", by the debug
  strings 0x0083c2d8..0x0083c324). `game/squad_controller.cpp` life cycle, update and tick, the order
  queue, the panel icons, the senses, the vehicles, saved games (chunk 0xCEF); `squad_actions.cpp` the
  action, the order queue's processing, the command module's squad commands, looting;
  `squad_combat.cpp` targets, weapons, bursts, stopping to fire, cover, the vehicles' guns, hits,
  events, stimuli, the alert preset; `squad_movement.cpp` the pace, stepping aside (the leader,
  grenades), keeping to the walking network, the map's attack points; `squad_order.{h,cpp}` the
  orders (S_HumanOrder) and their pool; shared helpers in `squad_internal.h`. 0x00524c60..0x0053b2c0,
  the orders 0x0058c540..0x0058d7e0, the pool 0x005825c0 / 0x00582640 / 0x00583980; names in
  `names/squad.txt` (the squad entries of human.txt / team.txt moved there), types in `46_human.spec`.
  * the action (S_SquadAction, +0x1E0): walk to the place (or the target) within the radius, turn
    toward it (cos 0.95), then the command; attacks and following go on (keepGoing); noCommand only
    walks there (with notifyModule the command module directs the next move). Bounding hops from
    cover to cover along a direction (FindCoverPoint: the network's cover points against it).
  * orders: kinds 1 move, 2 watch, 3 act (a squad command of the module on an actor), 4 follow the
    selected member, 6 / 7 wait for the module; each sets a task, the flags carry the pace, the
    stance and the AI mode. A marker frame and a pickable 0.4 x 1.6 m box stand on the command map;
    the path preview comes from the walking network (BuildPathPoints, __fastcall).
  * combat: the squad's target (the command module's), else the member's own (PickTarget; an
    attack's target preferred unless another is much nearer), modes 0 / 2 fire only when told; meleeTrackers
    within 7 / 30 m; armoured cars (+0x5E0) and animations (+0xD8 == 2) only with launchers. Bursts
    and pauses by the weapon's carry mode and the distance; walking members stop to fire now and
    then (GetBurstDefaults by behaviour); behaviour 5 lies down to fire; rifles are not fired
    hurrying; melee within 1.2 m (a knife from behind as command 0x24). Weapon preferences by
    the target, its distance (20 / 40 m) and indoors / outdoors (BuildWeaponPrefs bits).
    Gunners: the Junkers' at planes (0x005e8b70 the nearest air attack within 150 m in sight, else
    0x005f0e40 the Junkers 88 setup's), others lead humans by 1/8 s with 0.1 m scatter, aim at a
    car's bound centre; cannons fire 2 s bursts. Stimuli (hits, loud sounds, shots, sounds) are
    looked at by head (walking) or body, the farthest source first.
  Fixed: the destructor left the orders out of their pool; ReleaseHumanOrder of null;
  ProcessOrders with an empty queue; combat order 0xD with empty hands; the order's path preview /
  actor point with an empty path; TakeHit read a human's role from any attacker.
  Quirks kept: DropAction replaces all flags with the throw bit; CutWire refuses the second side
  when there is no way to the first; SwapUniform's test is inverted (it always fails); the watch
  order reads the queue's first slot after it was released (QueueWatchOrder takes it as `model`);
  PickCoverDir's loop has no effect; saving writes the default flags into the orders; LoadGame
  enters a chunk of a wrong id without leaving it; SnapToNetwork does nothing without a start
  (so OnGrenadeThrown's call does nothing); ChooseWeapon prints no weapon name; UpdatePace
  measures the leader's velocity and drops it.
  Found on the way: C_Actor slots 0xA4 / 0xA8 were swapped (now GetSeatWeaponAim, the direction,
  and GetSeatWeaponOrigin, the gun's position); I3D_driver slot 0xA8 GetHierarchyBound; the command
  module (C_auto_0080fb28; GetModule0089ad40 0x004563e0) slots GetSquadTarget, OnFriendlyFire,
  DelayFormationChecks, ExecuteCommand, Set / IsWhispering, OnMemberIdle, Unknown50 and
  OnOrderMoveDone (__fastcall, no human); C_FormationShape's slot functions 0x00598290..0x0059af30;
  C_Car HasLocalPlayerAboard / CanEnter / GetCrewSide / GetSeatEntry; C_Interface's order markers
  0x004b9080..0x004b9b10; C_SightModule::CanSee; C_Human::GetSquadStatusIcon / GetSquadModeIcon (were
  Unknown13C / 140). ret_audit: PickUp has a second argument (a supply box's item; the port had put
  the box pointer in the slot), SelfHeal and 0x00528570 take the (unused) time; the self-heal delay
  is RandFloat * 4000 + (100 - first aid) * 200 ms. S_AIFire's +0x14 chunk is now
  CHUNK_FIRE_LAST_TARGET (it clashed with the fire order's after the phase-2 rename).
  Not ported (no callers; tier 1): 0x00525bb0 / 0x00525c20 (cover by direction), 0x00530db0,
  0x005332d0, StepAsideFrom 0x005356e0, 0x00537d20 (the formation queries), UpdatePickedTarget
  0x00537f40 (the only writer of +0x238), AllocBodyCommand 0x00582550; the controllers' network
  messages 0x0053b2f0..0x0053b970 come with the player controller; the stimulus deque helpers
  0x00533880 / 0x00533de0 / 0x00533ea0 ... stay for their other callers.
  Tier 1 after the port (all decompiles exported again): 7882 functions, 1031 errors in 153/609
  files, 384 stubs (8080 / 1042 / 154 / 397 before). Note: one run kept a stale
  `src/tier1/f_00535e40.cpp` (old text of now-ported functions, 48 errors); a plain `gen_tier1.py`
  removed it. Check for tier-1 files whose functions are all in ported.txt after a port.
- controllers, phase 4: the enemy AI C_EnemyController and the sniper AI C_SniperController
  (2026-10-18; vtables 0x00811730 and 0x00811618, the latter the "C_Controller8" of earlier passes).
  `game/enemy_controller.cpp` life cycle, the AI modes, events, the choice of the task, firing,
  saved games (chunks 0x13B1..0x13B8 after the AI base's 0x139D); `enemy_tasks.cpp` the guard,
  search and morale broken tasks; `enemy_combat.cpp` the combat task and the walking network's
  points it fights from; `enemy_gunner.cpp` the gunner task and the post's static guns;
  `sniper_controller.cpp` the sniper (chunks 0x13BB..0x13BE); shared helpers in `enemy_internal.h`.
  0x005478c0..0x0055cb50 and the destructors 0x005a4460..0x005a4540; names in `names/enemy.txt`,
  types: the C_EnemyController, C_SniperController and C_PlayerController classes of
  `46_human.spec` (moved out of 90_auto.spec).
  * the enemy: one task at a time (guard the post, search, combat, morale broken, gunner) chosen
    by what it knows; the AI mode sets the alert range and the reaction delay (aggressive
    attacks at once, defensive waits for the enemy to come near or face it, zombie, also when
    crippled, is slow). Morale (STAT_MORALE, +2 per C_HumanEvents::GetMoraleGain) rises and falls
    with the events; broken (with enemies in view), a steady human with a weapon fights on, a
    zombie or one with an enemy within 15 m surrenders (hands up for 30 s at least), the others
    flee. Guards man a static gun near the post (FindGunToUse), take cover against the enemies
    in view, and a
    target shielded in a vehicle (a car or a static weapon's seat with SEAT_FLAG_HIDDEN) gets the
    launcher from a point in range, else they hide beside the vehicle (states 100..0x66,
    300..0x12E, shared with combat) or turn on the nearest enemy on foot. Searches walk to the
    event's source (heard, seen, hit; a stronger or a friend's event takes over), look around,
    idle 15..25 s, then go home and back to the script 2..5 s later. Combat keeps the weapon's range (GetWeaponRange) over cover
    points (CoverAgainst: the stances a point covers against a position; PickCoverPoint flags),
    approaches, flanks or retreats; grenades at the middle of the enemies 10..50 m away
    (PlanGrenade). Gunners lead humans (GetLeadPoint: 1/8 s of velocity, the body 0.3 m down),
    scatter by STAT_SHOOTING, sweep, wait (r*130+70)*nearest*0.5 ms between targets, leave a
    useless or unreachable gun.
  * the sniper: keeps to the post (C_HumanEvents::GetSniperPos / GetSniperRadius, the walking
    points within the radius; the radius is a float, the port had an int) and fires from the
    sniper points (checkpoint type 10) at the enemies in view, or turns to what it heard; a
    zombie sniper only turns and fires from where it stands. Some time after the last enemy or
    sound it goes back to the script. Morale flags from the initial STAT_MORALE (> 90 / > 75).
  The decompile was wrong in many of these functions (lost jumps that merged states, a jump table
  shown as one path, half of a filter loop's removals, float sums regrouped): every state machine,
  filter and x87 sum was checked in the disassembly; `tools/asm.py dis|flow|x87 ADDR` does that
  now (see its docstring for the compare + jump decoding).
  Fixed: the destructor left a search task's event out of its pool; a body heard of nobody known
  read a missing human; CanFireMoving with empty hands; a guard's gun gone from a saved game was
  read through null; IsOutOfGunReach with no vehicle (an event raised as the human left it);
  GunnerEvent tested the killer's health and reach before that he is a human, and read the
  health of any actor; PickGunnerTarget divided by a stimulus' 0 time left; FindApproachPoint's
  list could run below empty.
  Quirks kept: FindApproachPoint removes a point that fails both tests twice; IsLineOfFireFree's
  ray starts at the target's position and goes away from the gun; standing cover scores 0 in
  FindRetreatPoint (the standing bit times the weight); TakeCoverStance's second loop (crouching)
  cannot hold; FindCoverToward's 45-degree filter feeds nothing; FindFlankPoint's HorizontalCos
  result is dropped; NearestPoint's distance out-param is the last point's; UpdateCombat's 1..4 s
  countdown is replaced by 1 s right after; the sniper's `rand * 0 + 3000`, its states 1 / 2 are
  not reached and a type-5 task runs an empty function; m_gunCheckTime counts down but nothing sets
  it; SaveGame writes the morale check and the cover time twice; the gun check's next time is
  never read; the body's direction normalized twice in the sniper's turns.
  Found on the way: C_Actor slots 0x9C GetSeatWeaponRounds and 0xBC CanSeatWeaponAim(seat, pos);
  C_AIController::PlanGrenade (slot 0x110) returns bool; C_Script::HasOnAlarmDone;
  C_HumanEvents::GetMoraleGain (0x005ae940); 0x0041cfd0 ("empty_function") is S_vector::Square;
  CHECKPOINT_TYPE_SNIPER 10 / CHECKPOINT_TYPE_GUN 11; AXIS_RELOAD 13; C_ScriptedController::
  GetAlarmPos (+0x140). The 137 slots 90_auto.spec gave 0x00811730 ran into the player
  controller's vtable: C_PlayerController (0x00811848, 67 slots, 0x560 bytes, 48 fields from
  Ghidra's analysis) is now a spec class and its 48 functions are its members. Importing renamed
  vtables left the old name's `_func_<vtbl>__slot` types BAD (27 slots of the player's vtable);
  re-importing the class fixed them. ret_audit: the sniper's CheckDone pops a (unused) time.
  Not ported (no callers, no data references; tier 1): 0x00556870, 0x00556d70, 0x00557df0,
  0x0055a190 (the sniper's task-4 start); dead helpers dropped: HorizontalCos 0x00556610, the
  empty / always-true functions 0x00559650, 0x0054d560, 0x0055b6a0, 0x0055b6b0.
  The sniper's +0x150 (the stance at Attach, only saved) was named m_stance like the base's +0x4C
  (the port's shadowed it, tier 1's flattened struct declared it twice): now m_startStance.
  Absorbed 17 helpers (the event pool's copy / clear, vector insert / fill / tidy instantiations,
  the stimulus deque's pop, HorizontalCos and the empty functions).
  Tier 1 after the port (all decompiles exported again): 7705 functions, 989 errors in 150/609
  files, 370 stubs (7882 / 1031 / 153 / 384 before).
- controllers, phase 5: the scripted AI C_ScriptedController and the neutral AI C_NeutralController
  (2026-10-18; vtables 0x00811500 and 0x008113e8). `game/scripted_controller.cpp`
  0x0058d810..0x00591ee0 and the slots 0x005a4290..0x005a43e0; names in `names/scripted.txt` (the
  entries of human.txt moved there), types: the C_ScriptedController, S_ScriptAttack and
  C_NeutralController classes of `46_human.spec` (moved out of 90_auto.spec). Saved games: chunk
  0x13A7 (the state after the AI base's 0x139D), 0x13A8 / 0x13A9 the fire target / frame, 0x13AA,
  0x13AB the attack (0x13BA, 0x13BB), 0x13AC / 0x13AD the turn target.
  * the scripted AI carries out the HUMAN_* commands: moves to walking points at the script's
    pace (MoveTo; the script goes on at the move's label when the route ends), the stance, firing
    at an actor or a frame for a time (AttackActor / AttackFrame, also from a vehicle's seat
    weapon: GetSeatAimDir leads a human like the enemy's GetLeadPoint, aims 0.75 m above
    anything else, scatters by 6 % of the distance; a cannon fires within 1 degree, a gun within
    10), AttackMoveTo (S_Attack: every 0.5..3.5 s it looks for a target and, by the weapon's
    behaviour and the distance, fires walking, stops or crouches for base + 0..1.5 s), keeping a
    formation slot (the pace by the distance: beyond 12 m MOVE_PACE_MARCH with the weapon down,
    8..12 m the script's run, 2..4 m its walk, within 1 m its silence; 4..8 and 1..2 m keep
    the pace), waiting for the player, sitting (Sit, ActivityTurk "sedimturek"; a stand
    command then stands up instead), carried objects, the weapon, activities, Suspend (frozen,
    deaf; network message 0x16), SetAnimation (message 0x12; a looped one is kept for joining
    clients), TurnAt (also a vehicle's seat weapon, TurnSeatWeapon every frame).
  * the alarm: what the AI senses (enemy or body seen, body or friend heard, a sound of chapter
    0 / 1 / 2 / 6, a loud sound that is not a friend's, shot at, hit by types 1..3) raises the
    script's alarm (C_Script::OnAlarm) once, with its kind (ALARM_*) and position; move flag
    0x80000000 (MOVE_ALARMED) holds it until SetAlarm(false) or MoveToAlarm; SetAlarmType
    switches kinds off. The enemy AI taking over turns the kind and position into a stimulus.
    ResetAI hands the human to the AI of its role (enemy / sniper, neutral, squad for the NPC
    roles, the player's back to the player).
  * a meleeTracker's alert (event 0x10000017, raised by the meleeTracker AI 0x00811fd0 on the human near what it
    barks at) becomes a remembered sound of chapter 2 (gunfire) for the human and the humans
    near him (ProcessMeleeTrackerAlerts, every tick).
  * the neutral AI is the AI base without a Tick of its own and with empty OnKilledEnemy,
    OnEnemySeen, OnEnemyLost, OnLoudSound, OnSoundHeard, OnShotAt and OnHit.
  The decompile lost the attack decision's base times (300 / 600 / 1000 ms added to rand * 1500,
  and the modes per behaviour), read TurnAt's single direction as two vectors, and regrouped
  the aim sums; all was checked in the disassembly (tools/asm.py; it now traces FIADD & co.).
  Fixed: SetAnimation copied the name without bounds (and a null name through null); a loud
  sound with no source read its frame; the friend test of a loud sound asked any actor, as a
  human, about its friends; a nearby actor that is gone was read (ProcessMeleeTrackerAlerts); TakeWeapon
  with no long gun container; frame names loaded without a terminator.
  Quirks kept: TurnSeatWeapon tests the target against the unnormalized difference (a target
  farther than a metre passes the 0.985 test early) and normalizes the vehicle's way three
  times, and with no target keeps turning while pointing ahead; HasAIMode returns the masked
  flags' low byte (always false); HolsterWeapon asks the item's behaviour and drops it; the
  attack's STATE_20 is never set; m_alarmPos is not initialized; AttackActor keeps the old fire
  frame. The fire flags of ApplyWeaponControls are a byte with stale upper bytes in the
  original (the weapons read the low byte only).
  Found on the way: IsAISuspended reads +0x1D2 (Suspend), not +0x1D1 (seated); the four C_Human
  forwarders 0x005af1f0 / 0x005af410 / 0x005af550 / 0x005af6c0 have no callers (renamed
  ScriptedAdvanceScript, ScriptedHasAIMode, ScriptedPlaySpeech (speech group, chapter 9, from
  the body), ScriptedLookAround1); GetUnknown1B0 is the script's move flags (GetScriptFlags);
  C_HumanEvents::IsSniper; the AI-base functions that auto_members.json had given to subclasses
  (40, e.g. PickTarget to C_ScriptedController) are C_AIController's again. `tools/rename.py`
  wrote CRLF (human.h, human_controller.h, human_control.cpp had turned CRLF; fixed, and the
  files are LF again); it does not rename qualified names (pass `A::Old` itself).
  Absorbed: the LookAround thunk 0x00590740, the vector insert 0x00591f00.
  Tier 1 after the port: 7615 functions, 974 errors in 149/609 files, 360 stubs (7705 / 989 /
  150 / 370 before).
- controllers, phase 6: the player controller C_PlayerController and the remote controller
  C_RemoteController (2026-10-18; vtables 0x00811848 and 0x008112d8). `game/player_controller.cpp`
  (life cycle, HandleInput, the breath, the squad commands, comments, sight, hits, saved games, view
  modes, the medkit), `game/player_weapons.cpp` (firing, spread, recoil, reloads, the knife, charged
  throws, mines and car charges, the zoom, raising the weapon), `game/player_net.cpp` (the human's net
  object messages 0x0053b370..0x0053b990, the replication, the controller messages, the requests),
  `game/remote_controller.cpp`; shared bits in `game/player_internal.h`, the net object messages in
  `game/controller_net.h`. Names in `names/player.txt` (with the engine functions they call), types:
  C_PlayerController, C_RemoteController and S_ActionCommand in `46_human.spec` (the remote one moved
  out of 90_auto.spec); C_HumanController +0x54 is m_controlBits. Saved games: chunk 0x83F (the base),
  0x840 { +0x524, +0x525, the squad flags, the raised time, the throw delay, the combat order },
  0x841 { the pending command as the original's 0x40-byte object with its vtable, the delay }.
  * HandleInput: the HUD key, the controls a scripted camera / an interaction leaves (21-24, 26-31,
    35-38, 46-48, 62), the view (a vehicle seat at the controls looks along the human, a seat weapon
    takes the mouse, a hidden seat looks through its camera; the mouse turns by 0.3 / 0.1 through
    iron sights / 0.05 through scopes and binoculars, the pitch within 80 degrees; a car seat with
    SEAT_FLAG_ARMED and climbing keep within 60 degrees of the frame), the drop key, the weapon keys
    latched for the tick, the breath key, the paces (marching needs a standing human with endurance
    / 10 < stamina). The atan2 / sin / cos are the FPU's extended results: double here.
  * the network: a client replicates its human (position, view, rate = the walk's direction at the
    human's speed, the control bits) and sends actions, the dive and the binoculars through the net
    object; the server checks a client's fire against the weapon's attack delay (shotInterval * 100),
    carries the actions out and passes them on; the controller messages carry the inventory requests,
    commands (ExecuteCommand's result decides whether the clients get them), hits, healing, the
    soldier class (the skills), the equipment request (CTRL_MSG_21), fall damage (CTRL_MSG_17).
    A request of a client stamps the player's activity (S_NetPlayerState +0xA8, timeGetTime).
  * squad commands (single player): whispered or aloud (SetWhispering per command pair), carried out
    by the command module (ExecuteActionCommand) when the sentence ends (or after 1..1.5 s); 0x21 /
    0x22 call in the air attack / the artillery with the radios (item types 0xF3 "Radio-AirAttack",
    0xF4 "Radio-Artillery") at the ground 500 m along the view (the HeavyFireSupportModule 0x0089b2c0).
  The view modes: 1 binoculars (belt behaviour 14), 2 iron sights (scope aim mode 5), 3 scopes.
  Fixed: OnMessage2 read the move / transfer / pick-up containers from freed messages, leaked the
  control state message (and read it through null when the allocation failed) and the use-object
  message in a local game; the scope's field of view of a hands item that may be gone (TickLocal,
  SetViewMode); a missing hands item in HoldBreath and MeleeStrike, missing hands / head containers
  (HandleInput, OnHit, the charged throws); weapon fields read from any item (the drop key, the
  belt throw, the empty click, the zoom, ServerAction, the remote's actions); UpdateZoom read missing
  buttons; SayNear and OnHit took any actor for a human; the squad tables read past their end for
  commands over 0x3D; the remote controller with no net object; DesignateTarget read the aircraft
  attack values past a single player mission.
  Dropped (copy protection): WeaponControls (a failed check now and then dropped the weapon),
  ApplySpread (spread 50 past mission 8), SetViewMode (no sights / scope), UseMedkit (a deadly hit
  on the patient now and then).
  Quirks kept: OnMessage does not read types 5, 17, 18, 21 (HandleMessage 5, 17, 21; OnMessage2 1, 5,
  13, 14, 18, 19, 20; the remote 5, 17, 21) and loses step with the stream; OnHit queues the hit for
  the clients in single player too; the stored types of the take-from-box / transfer requests are
  wrong (their Write sends the right ones); the soldier class message prints a rise even when it went
  down (the player) or sets the skills from the old class (the remote); OnDeath decides the VIP
  objective against the first objective's side; the binoculars message keeps the client's mark
  when the server passes it on; the net object replicates while the movement's +0x1AC / +0x9C0 is set.
  Found on the way: C_HumanController::ExecuteCommand / UseActor / UseMedkit return bool (the
  server forwards a command only when it was carried out); the squad's slots 0x118 GetFlags and
  0x144 IsQuiet return values; the camera's slots 0x48 SetViewDir and 0x80 HasScriptedView; the
  HUD's 0x00492130 SetAimView(on, fov), 0x00491130 SetWeaponView, 0x00493500 CanUseWeapon; the
  command module's slots 0 ExecuteActionCommand, 0x0C HasGroupMembers, 0x34 ReleaseFollowers;
  C_NetGame 0x0072f890 GetPlayerStats / 0x0072fe90 SetPlayerClass; console command 6 is "heal"
  (it also takes the player's recoil away); the AIRCRAFTATACK values are read crossed (an axis
  caller gets the second).
  Not ported (no callers, no data references; tier 1): 0x0053cf80 (the nearest walking point of
  type 12 along a direction), 0x0053ed50 (a control state message), 0x0053efc0 (a controls message).
  Absorbed: the vector inserts 0x00547460 and 0x00547880.
  Tier 1 after the port: 7533 functions, 948 errors in 146/609 files, 344 stubs (7615 / 974 /
  149 / 360 before).
- controllers, phase 7: the squad's command module C_SquadCommander (2026-10-18; the object
  0x0089ad40, vtable 0x0080fb28 with 33 slots; `game/squad_commander.{h,cpp}`, 0x004559e0..0x0045b890).
  Names in `names/squad_commander.txt` (its entries left squad.txt, player.txt and team.txt); the class
  in `48_team.spec` (C_auto_0080fb28 renamed: class_names.json, rename_auto_structs.py, out of
  90_auto.spec; auto_members.json follows). S_ActionCommand's single player fields are `command`,
  `actor`, `value`, `member` (the pointers travel as 32-bit words in local games), and the HUD's
  commands are named (E_SquadCommand in controller_messages.h): 0x35 / 0x38 are the commands on
  actors (the player controller's "say" pair: the sentence is the actor command's).
  * the HUD's commands go to the member named or the idle members near the selected soldier
    (C_SquadController's follow, stop, bound, hold, the combat orders, engage, throw, launcher,
    follow me; 0x1B..0x20 to members with orders); the formation's types 0 / 1 / FORMATION_FOLLOW and
    its spacing (0..7, scale 0.75 * spacing + 1.5); the combat orders also go to the player
    controller's +0x548 (for when the soldier is handed to the squad AI).
  * take cover (8 / 9): five slots of a wedge 2 m ahead of the selected soldier along the direction;
    the cover points within 20 m (low ones, or with lying cover that way and a steep surface 0.7 m
    on), in its sector and within 2 m of the height of the walking point at the wedge, go to the
    nearest slot; with covers for at most half the members nobody moves, else spares fill the
    empty slots, then walking points; each slot's member faces 30 m along the direction (or turns
    off a standing cover), lying on low points; the rest stay, standing or crouching at random.
  * move near (10 / 11): the members leave static guns, take the wheel of driverless vehicles, and
    go to the places of a table in the frame of the selected soldier's view at the point.
  * commands on actors (ExecuteCommand): FilterMembers / PickMember choose by distance and kit
    (the door's key, the explosive, wire cutters 0x3B, a belt grenade, a medkit, a launcher for
    armoured targets, a uniform of the other side, a silenced weapon, the documents); the use of a
    ladder, a grenade into a room (0x12) and through a door (0x15) are manoeuvres: the members go to
    places by the actor's two walking points and S_SquadAction::notifyModule brings them back to
    OnOrderMoveDone for the next step (a closed door is opened first).
  * groups (Tick): 0 within 40 m or busy, 1 idle and near, 2 busy; 4 / 5 are computed; idle members
    say so (sentence 0x7F) 300..500 ms after each report waiting.
  Fixed: a null selected soldier (TakeCover, MoveNear); TakeCover's arrays and table overran past
  five members, and it read through null / before its lists when no walking point, slot, spare or
  member was within reach; MoveNear's places past its three tables; a ladder without two walking
  points; IsInGroup past the stored groups; the controllers of allied NPC team members taken for
  squad controllers (CollectFor, DelayFormationChecks, OnPlayerNoise, OnFriendlyFire, the idle
  reports); Reset keeps no groups / idle reports of the last mission's (deleted) humans.
  Quirks kept: SortByDistance swaps the members but not their distances (only the first is surely
  the nearest); FilterMembers skips the member after one it removes (the car case); a ladder
  manoeuvre with more than three members waits for ever; a door manoeuvre without the door's two
  walking points stays set with nobody sent.
  Found on the way: C_SightModule::TestNow returns bool; ApplyOrder, FilterMembers, PickMember,
  RemoveBusy and SortByDistance are __stdcall; CollectFor's last argument goes to a picker that
  returns null (0x004592d0); the debug hook 0x00456ef0 takes the tick's time; sentence 0x7F is the
  idle report.
  Not ported (no callers, no data references; tier 1): 0x0045ad40 (the cover point within a radius of a
  place against a target, by stance flags, away from given places, the nearest or the farthest),
  0x0045b110 (the same among the points reachable from the place), 0x0045b4e0 (keeps a position
  within a walking point's radius); the place table 0x0089ac90 is read by nobody (MoveNear runs into
  it past its 6th member).
  Absorbed: eight vector helpers 0x0045c410..0x0045d030.
  Tier 1 after the port: 7461 functions, 946 errors in 144/605 files, 344 stubs (7533 / 948 /
  146 / 344 before).
- the heavy fire support (2026-10-18; "HeavyFireSupportModule", module 16 (MODULE_HEAVY_FIRE_SUPPORT),
  the object 0x0089b2c0 of 0x1B4 bytes; `game/heavy_fire_support.{h,cpp}`, 0x00470500..0x004736d0).
  C_HeavyFireSupport = I_HeavyFireSupport (vtable 0x00810050: RequestAirAttack, RequestArtillery;
  the pure base 0x00810058) + C_Module at +4 (vtable 0x00810010: Tick, Update, StartMission,
  StopMission, OnMessage); C_AirStrike (0x80 bytes) and C_ArtilleryStrike (0x40). Names in
  `names/heavy_fire_support.txt` (the old entries left player.txt), types in `50_fire_support.spec`
  (the C_Module part as C_HeavyFireSupport_Module; the auto classes renamed). S_AirAttackRequest +0x14
  is the number of planes (1..3), not a delay.
  * the server (RequestAirAttack / RequestArtillery, the player's radios): one strike of each kind
    per team at a time (flags +0x1B0 [team] air, [2 + team] artillery; StartMission clears them);
    results 0 sent, 1 busy, 2 not the server (the artillery always returns 2), 3 a kind past 16.
    The clients get a module message (bit 0 air: target, direction, kind, planes, caller; 1
    artillery: target, caller) and run their own copy (team 2). Update runs them every frame,
    Tick only on a machine without a client side.
  * an air attack: 1..3 planes of the kind (tablesirsupport.tbl, 16 kinds: model, speed 30 m/s,
    engine sound group, 2 bombs per plane, damage 1000, range 10 m; the file is not in the unpacked
    game data, so the defaults are unverified) come in along the direction 60 m up and are over the
    target after 15 s; the server's explosion goes off on the ground below (S_Explosion, the caller
    as its source), the clients scatter bombs x planes blasts (effects 0x2B / 0x30 / 0x31, explosion
    sounds) within the range, 0..300 ms apart; it ends 15 s later. The planes hang on one another
    (the odd ones 1.5 bound radii aside and back, the even ones 3 to the other side) and weave
    (sine / cosine of the time). The caller's radio says 90005601.wav, then 90005603.wav.
  * artillery: 7 s after the call the shells hit (an explosion of range 20 m, damage 1000 on every
    side), the clients get 20 blasts 0..1000 ms apart; it ends 7 s later. Radio: 90005600, 90005602.
  Fixed: the clients' artillery strikes (team 2) clear the byte after the four flags when they end,
  the low byte of g_vertexAnimModule's vtable pointer at 0x0089b474 (a crash of network clients
  waiting to happen); flags of other teams past the four; a message's kind past the table and a
  caller id without an actor; an air attack without planes (Update read the first of none); values
  before the first kind and kinds past 16 in airsupport.tbl.
  Quirks kept: the caller's id in the messages is reversed (-1 for a caller; without one the
  original reads through null: Fix, -1), so remote clients never hear the radio; the last plane does
  not weave; a timer that lands exactly on 0 strikes twice; the clients' air attacks clear the
  artillery flag of team 0; the model name is sprintf's format; the table is read at static
  initialisation; the destructor leaves the strikes to StopMission.
  Found on the way: S_SoundRequest flags 0xD for the engines (silent to AI, slot kept, loop);
  GetDubbingSampleSize(90005601) gives the radio's length; frame +0x3C is the bound's radius.
  Absorbed: the vector inserts 0x004717b0 / 0x004729c0 / 0x00473070 and helpers; the exception
  unwind code's helpers 0x00471080, 0x00471790, 0x00472490, 0x004724b0, 0x00472980, 0x00472990.
  Tier 1 after the port: 7407 functions, 944 errors in 143/598 files, 343 stubs (7461 / 946 /
  144 / 344 before).
- HUD part 1, the first-person view (2026-10-18; `game/first_person_view.{h,cpp}`, 0x00490740..
  0x00493610, C_game +0x5C). What earlier ports called the "HUD panel" (C_HudPanel) is the original's
  C_FirstPersonView (its debug strings): the active soldier's hands and weapon in front of the camera,
  clients only. Renamed everywhere (src, names, Ghidra: /rename_data_type, then the stray empty
  C_HudPanel deleted); C_game's ShowHud / IsHudShown / ShowHudPanel / IsHudPanelShown are now
  ShowFirstPersonView / IsFirstPersonView / ShowBinocularsView / IsBinocularsView: the game's "HUD"
  option (g_hudOption) is this view, the "panel" the binoculars' view. Names in `names/hud.txt`
  (the old entries left game.txt, player.txt, controller.txt, items.txt), types in `51_hud.spec`
  (C_FirstPersonView 0x4C370 bytes, S_FpvAnim, S_FpvStage).
  * the model: a copy of the hands ("FPV_hands", or the uniform's fpv model without its extension;
    "w_default" ones use FPV_hands) with the weapon's fpv model and magazine in it, linked to the
    scene's first-person root; hidden for a car's driver and in the binoculars' view, off while the
    soldier is dead. ApplySleeves gives the hands' "ARM" / "ARS" materials the sleeve texture of the
    soldier's model. Light level from the body (GEffects slot 0x54); the lens flare is off while the
    view is on (GEffects slot 0xBC SetLensFlareOn).
  * animations (tables\FpvAnims.sav, read at Init: 0x3039 { 100 + item type { 2000 + event (13) {
    variants { 1000 { file }, int ratio } } } }; checked against the unpacked file: 52 of the 255 item
    chunks have animations; event names from the files: 0 Idle1, 1 Idle1LST, 2 Shot, 3 ShotLST,
    4 / 5 AimShot, 6 Reload, 7 Jammed, 8 Arm, 9 Disarm, 10 DisarmLST, 11 Aim, 12 Daim; E_FpvEvent).
    A variant is picked by a random 0..99 against cumulative ratios (the Colt's 70 / 90 leave 9 %
    unplayed, with a debug message); the playing one blends out at 5 per second on stages 0..3. Shots
    flash the "flash" frame for 50 ms (scale 0.7..1.1, random roll); the Panzerfaust (item 38) hides
    its "ammo" frame (the warhead) when fired. Sounds at the camera: a grenade's throw, a weapon drawn.
  * the view: the model sways with the soldier's speed (sine of a phase advancing 2 per second, x87
    FSIN in double), follows the camera's position and direction, dropped by the pose (E_FpvPose:
    lowered -0.2, raised, the weapon put away to -0.8 for a belt grenade, the grenade, the weapon
    back); the camera's fields of view ease to the view's at pi/1000 per ms (the game's, 65 degrees
    for the second; through the sights the weapon's and 40, the camera's special mode 7, the
    interface's view mode (iron sights at once, the scope when its animation ends); binoculars 10).
  Fixed: LoadAnimTable wrote wherever the chunk ids pointed; a missing "ammo" frame, uniform fpv model
  name, hands weapon (OnInventoryEvent's idle) or item definition (the draw sound); PlayAnim outside
  the table. Quirks kept: the weapon-back pose snaps to -0.2 at once toward a lowered weapon (a test
  the wrong way round); the flash timer settles at 0 and switches the flash off every frame; through
  the sights the last frame stays instead of the idle animation; LoadGame returns false; ratios short
  of 100 can pick nothing; the error message of a missing table is formatted and dropped.
  Found on the way (fixed in earlier ports): C_HumanController::Unknown587530 (0x00587530, declared
  empty) returns the weapon stance (2 raised, 1 lowered; GetWeaponStance, E_WeaponStance) and
  C_HumanWork's request "unknown5C" is the weapon raised; C_CameraModule::GetCameraPos / GetCameraDir
  write an S_vector4 (callers passed S_vectors); camera slots 0x50..0x60 SetFov / GetFov /
  GetViewDistance / SetFov2 / GetFov2 (`names/camera.txt`, the vtable typed in Ghidra: slot names
  come from symbols.json, `import_spec.py --structs C_CameraModule_vtbl`); the camera's special mode 7
  is the sights' view (CAMERA_MODE_AIM); I3D_material slots 0x1C SetTexture / 0x44 ResolveTexture,
  +0x0C the texture (I3D_texture +0x18 its file); I3D_mesh_level +0x58 the face groups; I3D_model
  slot 0x90 (Unknown90, after SetAnimTime(stage, 0)); C_Interface 0x0049dc60 SetViewMode;
  C_AnimatedItemDef::GetFpvMagazine; C_ItemDef::GetFpvModelName.
  Tier 1 after the port: 7364 functions, 920 errors in 142/597 files, 332 stubs (7407 / 944 /
  143 / 343 before).
- HUD part 2, the interface core, the compass, the message log and the mission information / scores
  (2026-10-18; `game/interface.{h,cpp}`, `interface_compass.cpp`, `interface_messages.cpp`,
  `interface_info.cpp`, `game/text_box.h`; 0x00493610..0x0049fef0). C_Interface (C_game +0x58,
  0x6B900 bytes) left world.h for `game/interface.h`, which also declares the later parts' panels with
  their addresses and opaque storage of the original's size; names in `names/hud.txt`, types in
  `51_hud.spec` (C_StatusText renamed C_PauseText in Ghidra; C_TextBox moved there from
  `90_auto.spec`: C_Interface embeds it by value and tier 1 emits the auto classes last).
  * the core: its own scene (viewport the whole screen), the "gameinterface" model (driver states 0x15
    off and 0x19 on while it loads), an orthogonal camera at (0.5, -0.375, -15.5), a white light
    "GI_Light", the panels' click "button.wav" (g_clickSound); the icons are the textures of the
    model's hidden "playerstate1_%02d" (20), "playerstate2_%02d" (40) and "action%02d" (39) visuals.
    Render / Update / HandleInput as the original orders them; the keys named from the default
    bindings (INPUT_* 19..45 in game_internal.h). The lower panel's three heights (-1.28972 up,
    -1.321 low, -1.375 down); the explosive timer moves out of the signals' / command map's way.
    The pick: a ray along the camera's view from the soldier's "cameraFPV" / "camera3RD" dummy (1.5 m
    ahead in a vehicle) as far as the view reaches, skipping water and what bullets pass; behind water
    or a shoot-through part a second ray finds a human (usable within 2 m); doors only within 0.65 m;
    usable within 0.2 m or when the roll menu has actions; single player every 100 ms, the last actor
    kept for 4 misses. The pick outline: corner marks around the actor's screen box (scene slot 0x98
    TransformPoints, line list). Saved game: chunks 1000 (commanded member, command map open), 0x3E9
    (the hints, 0x1F5), 0x3EA (16 bytes), 0x3EB (the stance panel's timer).
  * the compass: the needle is a spring toward the soldier's view (pull sin(heading - needle) / 100
    per ms, friction 3 %, at most 0.35), the card turns with it, the arrow with the steps' wobble
    (a leading-foot change kicks it); eight sound marks (blue footsteps, red the rest) flash 500 ms;
    the radar's blips (squad, seen enemies) fade in / out, off at difficulty 4 and in network games.
  * the message log: the last 50 lines aged every 2 s (older ones a line higher per step), the history
    (single player pauses), the chat line ("Say: " / "SayTeam: " or a console command; the unicode
    font's UTF-8 input and backspace), keys repeating after 150 ms then every 50; glyph quads of the
    font manager's font 3 drawn as pre-transformed triangles.
  * the information / scores ("ingame_info", 73 text styles embedded from 0x008399f8): single player
    the objectives and the scripts' hints with key tags replaced; deathmatch / occupation / objectives
    / co-op the players (score column = the round's, stats[3]; kills, deaths, stats[4] not identified;
    rank names by the uniform's side), the teams side by side (allies left), the server, the time left
    and the point limit; redrawn every 500 ms. S_NetPlayerState stats: 1 kills, 2 deaths, 3 score, 5
    rank (what SetPlayerClass sets, texts 0x2792 / 0x279C short names).
  Fixed: C_InfoScreen::Init crashed without "ingame_info" / "game_info" (now false); a missing
  soldier crashed Update's speedometer, UpdateCrosshair and UpdatePick; Init leaked the model when it
  failed; the compass' sound mark index could reach 8; the hints' loader looped forever on a partial
  id; the chat line's UTF-8 backspace tested the byte before the buffer on a line of continuation
  bytes. Dropped: the MD5 of the executable stamped into saved-game chunk 0x3EA (0x006ea340; nothing
  checks it; the port writes back what it loaded). Quirks kept: only the last member's removal takes
  its blip off the radar; the map and the scores draw twice when forced; an empty history scrolls up;
  a hit without an actor behind see-through geometry replaces the pick's distance; LoadGame does not
  leave unknown chunks; the chat line's scroll counts the input's characters, the drawing those with
  the "Say: " prefix.
  Found on the way: C_RollMenu::HasActions is a member (thiscall, this unused), its Init takes a third
  argument (the command map's own instance), its HandleInput and the map cursor's take held / pressed,
  five panels' Update take timeDelta (ret_audit); C_SignalsPanel::Open's second argument is the target
  actor; the speedometer gets km/h, rpm (C_Vehicle 0x004f4c50) and fuel / tank (C_Vehicle +0xF5C /
  +0x2220); C_Human::GetPlayerViewMode 1..3 hide the crosshair; script_module's "panel" is the command
  map (IsCommandMapOpen / OpenCommandMap); IGraph slots 0x44 / 0x74 / 0x78 / 0x88 / 0xA0..0xB0,
  I3D_camera 0x84 / 0x88, I3D_light 0x84 / 0x90, I3D_scene 0x98 (all re-imported, ls3d vtables match).
  Left to tier 1: the unreferenced rows 0x00498a20 / 0x00498c50 / 0x00499250 / 0x00499480 and
  C_TextBox::C_TextBox 0x00496cc0 (the text library, 0x0063de40..0x0064d060, declared in text_box.h).
  Tier 1 after the port: 7183 functions, 913 errors in 142/591 files, 333 stubs (7364 / 920 /
  142 / 332 before; on the way the tier-1 header broke on C_Interface embedding C_TextBox, the callers
  of g_clickSound kept their untyped decompiles and 33 decompiles still named the old IGraph / scene
  slots: re-exported).
- C_str / C_chunk leftovers: the out-of-line copies are absorbed into the existing port (C_str copy
  constructor 0x004145b0, assignment 0x004145d0, mutable operator[] 0x0041d670, the concatenation's buffer
  setup 0x00414550; C_chunk::Descend 0x00415c90, the C_str string reader 0x00415d00 (ReadString + Ascend),
  WriteStringChunk 0x004256a0).
- game profile and campaign tree (`game/profile.{h,cpp}`, `game/campaign.{h,cpp}`; 0x006b4990..0x006c02f0,
  the node base 0x0066eef0..0x0066f010, C_GameProfile::GetTextDirectory 0x0048fbc0; names in
  `names/profile.txt`, types in `49_profile.spec`; 2026-10-18). C_GameProfile (g_gameProfile 0x008aea0c,
  0x34 bytes, vtable 0x00815a08 with GetLanguage) owns the campaign (+0x04), game.cfg, the HUD option,
  the soldier roster (HumanStore.def), the mission squad, two record lists, the player profile (was
  "C_MissionInfo"), the multiplayer settings and the play mode (+0x28); the language comes from the
  registry ("language", default english) and picks the unicode menu font (japanese, russian or a
  Text\<lang>\FontConfig.hxml). Saved games: chunk 1000 { 0x3E9 campaign, 0x3EA roster, 0x3EB squad,
  0x3EC / 0x3ED records, 0x3EF the script module's 256 values, 0x3F0 player, 0x3F1 play mode (0x225C),
  0x3F2 HUD (0x1642) }; a running game's load skips the campaign, roster, first records and play mode.
  The play mode S_PlayMode (vtable 0x008159f4): +4 singleMission, +8 mode (E_PlayMode 1 campaign CAM_,
  2 lone wolf LOW_, 3 carnage CAR_; save names "CAM_%02d%02d.sav", quick saves "CAM_quick.sav" /
  "SMCAM_quick.sav" in PlayersProfiles\<player>\); world.h's S_CampaignState / g_campaignState was this
  object (its `campaign` is singleMission, `mission` is mode) and is gone. The campaign tree
  (GameData\Gamedata%02d.gdt, one campaign each): C_CampaignNode (vtable 0x00815690, 10 slots: dtor,
  LoadDefinition, Load, Save, LoadProgress, SaveProgress, Next, Prev, GetCurrent, Reset), C_CampaignVideo
  0x00815668, C_CampaignScreen 0x00815b38 (kind 3: the equipment screen and its items), C_CampaignPlay
  0x00815b10, S_MissionObjective 0x00815a48 / S_MpObjective 0x00815ae8, C_CampaignContainer 0x00815a70
  (children + cursor; slots 2..5 are the children's Load / Save / progress, + Clear, GetCursorState,
  GetChild), C_CampaignRoot 0x00815b60, C_CampaignOperation 0x00815b94, C_MissionDef 0x00815a0c (+
  CreateObjective, AddObjective), C_MpMissionDef 0x00815aa8 (+ AddMpObjective; the session map's mission:
  objectives, team balance, presets, items), C_Campaign 0x00815a00 (0xF4: the campaigns, the MP mission,
  the single mission, the current campaign, version 2; LoadGame / SaveGame slots). Three formats: the old
  definition format (chunks 2 operation, 3 mission, 4 objective, 5 video, 6 screen, 7 play, 1000 strings),
  the full format the shipped files use (version chunk 3 = 2; 0x0A video, 0x14 screen, 0x1E play, 0x32
  mission, 0x3C operation, ...) and the progress of saved games (0x50 .. 0x54). C_ScriptModule::
  WriteSaveGameValues / ReadSaveGameValues 0x00427010 / 0x00427050 (inlined C_chunk::Write / Read of the
  values) are ported too. Quirks kept: an objective's Save resets its status to inactive;
  SetupSingleMission returns false also when it worked; mission 10's equipment goes to its first screen;
  a single mission's progress is rebuilt on load, not read; a .gdt that is not recognized stays open and
  ends the loading. Fixed: LoadFiles leaked the root of such a file; C_Campaign::GetObjectiveStatus read
  before the campaigns for campaign 0; LoadGame's old-layout path indexed an empty campaign list; the
  registry value was not terminated; SelectPlayer passed the profile name as a format string. Declared,
  not ported: C_GameConfig, S_HudOption, C_HumanStore, S_MissionSquad, C_ProfileRecords, C_Diary,
  C_Debriefing, C_PlayerProfile (their member functions are named in profile.txt). Not ported (no
  callers, no data refs): C_Campaign::SaveFiles 0x006bb540 (writes the campaigns back as .gdt files),
  0x006b7330 (PlayersProfiles\<player>\quick.sav; returns a released string). Ghidra: the auto classes
  of the vtables above renamed in place (class_names.json, auto_members.json); C_Campaign / C_MissionDef /
  S_MissionObjective keep their names (the auto twins are C_X__auto, unused) and moved from 10_core.spec
  to 49_profile.spec (the MP fields of the old S_MissionObjective / C_MissionDef are S_MpObjective's /
  C_MpMissionDef's); g_gameProfile is typed C_GameProfile, so the ~800 DAT_008aea10..34 references read
  g_gameProfile.m_x (the label g_campaignState was renamed away first); S_MultiplayerSettings' map vector
  is at +0x280 (was +0x284) and currentMap at +0x290 (was +0x294), size 0x298. The auto class of 0x00815b94
  had swallowed the next vtable 0x00815bc8 (a C_GameState subclass, 0x00688330 ...): those 12 functions now
  take a C_GameState `this`. Validated with a MinGW harness (campaign.cpp + chunk.cpp + c_str.cpp, stub
  pending.h / rw_data.h) on GAMEDATA\Gamedata00/01.gdt: both load and re-save byte-identical (22161 /
  11238 bytes); NextStep walks HD2's 8 operations to the outro and credits (112 steps); the progress
  (cursors, objective states) survives SaveGame / LoadGame inside chunk 0x3E9; single missions get the
  operation's earlier equipment (mission 10: on screen 2). Screen kinds by position: 1 before three
  briefings, 2 after an operation's intro, 3 the first equipment, 4 a later mission's equipment, 5 after
  a mission, 6 an operation's end (SS). The MP map list (GAMEDATA\mpmaplist.txt, parser 0x006189f0;
  C_MpMap 0x48 bytes, now in 10_core.spec with S_MpMapObjective / S_MpMapItem): +0x0C / +0x10
  AIRCRAFTATACK axis / allied (the MP mission's +0x50 / +0x54; the enemy controller 0x005467c0 reads them
  per side), +0x14 / +0x18 BALANCED axis / allied (+0x5C / +0x58: BalanceTeams(axis, allied)), OBJECTIVE
  {axis_text, allied_text, value, type="VIP" 1, side AXIS 0 / ALLIED (or none) 1, vip_uniform_id}.
  `import_spec.py --structs` now also emits the one-line vector_T helpers a definition uses (C_MpMap's
  new vectors had become 1-byte placeholders).
- application state machine (`game/game_app.{h,cpp}`, C_GameApp 0x006c0a70..0x006c4f10; names in
  `names/game_app.txt`, layout in `10_core.spec`; 2026-10-18). C_GameApp (g_gameApp 0x008aee24, 0x44
  bytes; the class moved out of pending.h) holds one C_GameState. Init: the profile, the last player,
  then the logos, or the console of the command line (`-cmd <command>`; `-c <script>` hides the
  window, runs "exec <script>" and prints the console banner). The intro: C_MovieState::Play(file,
  sequence) and GAME_STATE_MOVIE_FINISHED: Logo1 -> Logo2 -> Logo3 -> Intro -> the main menu
  (sequence 0 returns to the state the movie played over, 6 ends the game). StartStep turns a
  campaign step into a state: video -> C_VideoState (type 7), screen kinds 0 C_Kind0Menu (9), 1/2
  C_SquadMenu (3), 3/4 C_EquipmentMenu (4), 5 C_DebriefingMenu (5), 6 C_OperationEndMenu (14) (the
  menus' roles inferred from the campaigns; they share one implementation, the base C_MenuState,
  vtable 0x00815850), play -> C_game started with the current mission, none -> C_MainMenu (2).
  UpdateServer: one 100 ms tick per frame (the rest catches up later); the server's end of an update
  sends GAME_MSG_SESSION, the client's flushes the queued commands (PostCommand's fourth argument is
  `flush`, not text). UpdateClient: input (C_Controls::GetValues), Update, CommitPendingState, then the
  state value: 2 the next step (a Lone Wolf campaign skips the squad screens of kind 1), 3 the
  previous screen, 4 quit, 7 / 8 the session menu (joining / hosting), 9 the session's next map (the
  players on the loading screen as a per-side title text, +0x54 of the player state, and the name),
  10 the main menu, 11 the lobby (from a running game: the mission ends), 12 a movie over the state
  (the main menu's or the game's AVI; credits.bik), 13 the movie ended, 14 / 15 / 16 / 17 the in-game
  menu opens (single player: paused) / closes / ends the mission / opens in a network game. Commands:
  restart, save, load (the sound is off for 100 ms after a load or a restart; the profile's volume
  returns: C_PlayerProfile::ApplyVolume), load from the main menu (RequestLoad: a quick save goes
  straight into its mission, any other save continues the campaign: HD2 missions up to 22 and SS up to
  7 advance, SS 8 takes one step). Saved games: chunk 0x4E8C { 2999 the mission number (Sabre
  Squadron's count on after HD2's; 2998 accepted), 1000 the profile, the game }; a load needs a save
  of the mission played; the saves between missions hold the profile only. The diary is locked
  (C_Diary +0x00: AddText adds nothing) while a saved game is written / read and while a mission
  restarts. HasQuickSave / GetQuickSaveMission (any chunk id above 0x4E8B passes; a dead mission
  count), PrintMessage (the game's message lines; the console for a dedicated server or a shown
  console state). Fixed: StartStep started the state it had just deleted again for an unknown screen
  kind (now the main menu); the unbounded sprintfs ("exec %s", the loading screen's lines); the
  console's first word went into 32 bytes; PrintMessage passed the message as the format. Dropped:
  the copy protection (its bookkeeping in the loads; from the third mission on a failed check sent the
  campaign back to its start). Not identified: IGraph slots 0xDC (1 when a client starts, 0 for the
  console) / 0xE4 (Close), C_GameApp +0x08 / +0x0C / +0x10 and +0x12 (the state is not updated
  while set; nothing sets it), the random value +0x1C. Ghidra: the state type ids and values in
  game_state.h (GAME_STATE_BACK, JOIN / HOST_SESSION, MOVIE_FINISHED, CLOSE_GAME_MENU,
  MENU_END_MISSION; IsActiveState: running, starting or ending), C_GameState is 16-byte aligned (the
  states are _aligned_malloc'd); C_VideoState / C_MovieState specs (the 12 functions of vtable
  0x00815bc8 that the profile port typed as C_GameState are theirs); the auto classes renamed in place:
  C_MainMenu 0x00815e30, C_EquipmentMenu 0x00815db0, C_EquipmentMenuBase 0x008157d8, C_MenuState
  (was C_Class_815850), C_ConsoleState 0x008165a8; class_names.json has the other screens; the inline
  GetTypeId / destructors of the menus emitted in this range are named, not ported.
- profile layer (2026-10-18; names in `names/profile.txt` / `names/config_file.txt`, types in
  `49_profile.spec` and `46_human.spec`): the game profile's objects, 0x0069f9a0..0x006b4990 but the
  multiplayer settings' methods (0x006acb60..0x006ae5b0: map list, mpdesc.txt; they need the session
  menus' map classes).
  * soldier roster (`game/profile_roster.cpp`; S_SoldierProfile moved with the other soldier records
    from human.h to the new `game/soldier.h`): C_HumanStore (C_GameProfile +0x10) loads
    tables\HumanStore.def (chunk 0x29A with a chunk 9000 per soldier: names 0x2396 / 0x23A0 / 0x23AA,
    face 0x23F0, ints 0x26DE / 0x26F3..0x26F5, the stats block 0x26AD (+0x44 and 9 floats), a list
    0x26FC, +0x6C and the death flag 0x2580 in saved games; old formats 0x238C "first (nick) last",
    0x24B8 / 0x2648 records, 0x26AC int stats), the player's usernames.sav custom names (only written
    when a soldier has one), GetStats (health and strength as they are, the others but morale scaled by
    1 + 0.03 * level, at most 100), the rank names table g_rankNames (0x008ae960) and 24 text ids
    (0x008ae900). Validated: the shipped HumanStore.def (65 soldiers) loads and writes back
    byte-identical. FixNameEncoding (unicode font) only converts 'í' to UTF-8; other non-ASCII
    names stay.
  * mission squad (`game/profile_squad.cpp`): its soldiers are S_SquadSoldier (0xAC: an
    S_SoldierState and the load S_SoldierLoad: capacity = strength, weights / volumes of the belt
    (12 at most) and of the backpack's container, C_BackpackComp). An item's volume is twice its
    inventory size (1 for size 0). PutItem / RemoveItem / CountItems by slot bits (head 1, uniform 2,
    hands 4, backpack 8, long gun 0x10, belt 0x20, backpack contents 0x40), merging / splitting
    stacks, ReturnItems into the second item stock; chunk 10 per soldier with chunks 0x0B..0x17.
    S_SoldierState gained +0x34 / +0x38 (new squad soldiers: -1); the ported callers use
    std::vector<S_SquadSoldier *>.
  * `game/profile_misc.cpp`: C_ItemStock (was C_ProfileRecords; C_GameProfile +0x18 / +0x1C: item
    counts by type; chunk 0x4D2 with the type, the count after the chunk), C_GameConfig (game.cfg
    NETCDKEY / PARENTLOCK / LASTPROFIL; read from the exe's directory, written to the working
    directory), S_HudOption, C_Debriefing, C_MovieList (the extras' 17 movies, which were seen;
    chunks 0x1EC8 / 0x1EC9), C_Diary (+0x00 locked, the diary and the current mission's texts;
    chunks 0x6F / 0x70 with 0x71, old 0 / 2).
  * key bindings (`game/key_bindings.{h,cpp}`, C_PlayerProfile +0xEC): S_KeyBinding {action, device
    1 keyboard / 2 mouse, key, direction, mode 1 all / 2 / 3, group, text, changed}, the two default
    layouts (arrows, W A S D; 92 bindings each, recovered from the builders' code), conflicts (same
    key in an overlapping mode; two actions may share P), the menus' key names (texts, DirectInput
    names, IGraph slot 0xD0 GetKeyName) and the texts' <Move_forward> .. <Select_soldier_4> tags.
  * player profile (`game/player_profile.cpp`): profile.sav (chunk 0x1EDC: name, difficulty,
    progress per campaign and mode, last save and its text "mode$mission$date", five save slots,
    the movies), the old profile.pr, Options.bin (0x25A0 version 0x141, 0x25A6 the key bindings,
    0x25A5 the options C_PlayerOptions: was C_DetailSettings, now with the mouse sensitivity, gamma
    (IGraph slot 0x12C SetGammaRamp), volumes, a voice value, the detail switches, invert mouse,
    crosshair), the profile directories (unicode font: Profile_<n> and the name map
    g_profileDirectories), the last save's text and slots, LoadGameTexts.
  * `common/config_file.{h,cpp}` (C_ConfigFile, GetGameDirectory, SetWorkingDirectory) and
    `common/os_files.{h,cpp}` (FileExists & co, GetDateString).
  Fixed: S_SquadSoldier::SetProfile only range-checked negative indexes (never failing) and used a
  missing profile; unbounded copies (UpdateNames' 256-byte sprintfs, the old full name into 128
  bytes, the config file's value / line buffers, the last save's text); a truncated key-binding chunk
  looped forever; several loaders left a chunk they had not entered; the key name, the selected
  profile name and the scanned directory names were C_fstr formats; GetName formatted a null string
  for an unknown name. Quirks kept: config keys match anywhere in a line ("LASTSAVE" finds
  "LASTSAVETEXT="), the profile.pr text falls back to the last save's name, SetDefaultOptions copied
  an uninitialised int (now 0), RenameProfileName loses the mapping when the new name is taken.
  Not ported (no callers): S_SquadSoldier::SaveToFile / LoadFromFile (0x006a4350 / 0x006a4650),
  ForgetProfileName (0x006af0f0), C_GameProfile::GetOldQuickSaveFileName (0x006b7330). The video
  player (C_VideoState 0x00688330..) stays with the menus: its subtitles and the credits are menu
  text boxes and fonts.
  ret_audit --src: a member whose address comment says "(__fastcall" was a __fastcall function with
  `this` in ECX; "added by the port" marks parameters the original did not have.
  Test harness: `_re/harness/profile/build.bat` (MinGW, stub headers) builds the layer's files on
  their own and runs the roster round trip on HumanStore.def plus save / load round trips of the
  squad, item stock, diary, movies, key bindings, profile.sav, Options.bin and game.cfg.
  Tier 1 after the port: 8629 functions, 1034 errors in 155/618 files, 388 stubs (1039 / 156 / 392
  before). The type generators (ls3d_ghidra.py, typespec.py) now know wchar_t (IGraph::GetKeyName);
  it used to come out as an empty struct in ghidra_types.h and broke tier1_types.h.
- RET-n audit of every module (2026-10-17, `tools/ret_audit.py`: names prototypes, typed vtable
  slots and src/ declarations against the code's RET n). Ported code that dropped used values:
  the script VM's wire cut passed only the direction (`C_WireCut::Cut(dir, hideBrokenPart)`, the VM
  passes true: the door's broken part is hidden), InitInputAndTexts computed the language and
  dropped it (`LoadGameTexts(language)`), `AllocRememberedSound(chapter, pos, source, time)` was
  declared with the S_HeardSound instead of the source. Unused arguments the callers pass are now
  in the declarations and passed: C_Actor::Reactivate(server), C_HumanEvents::Add/RemoveNearbyActor
  (+distance), C_HumanEquipment::FindJoints(frame), C_HumanMorph::Relink(frame), S_HumanBody::
  Unknown5ac470 -> C_HumanMovement::Unknown7865c0 -> C_HumanWeaponPose::Unknown7a8760(arg),
  Unknown7ae7f0(carry set), AddBloodPool(+int), C_PlayerController::OnDamaged(type, zone, dir),
  C_Script::OnUse(int), C_TSExplosion::Trigger(int), C_Mine::OnSteppedOn(human, -1),
  C_HumanWork::SetStanceTimer(+blocked: 8 / 9 / m_blockedTimer), GiveItem(+int),
  C_ParticleEmitter::Draw(time), ToggleEmission(handle, 0), C_BulletShot::InitClient(+flare),
  SurfaceMarks_NewMark(1), C_CarryAnimTable(index), C_chunk::Ascend(uint32_t *size = nullptr)
  (stores the written chunk's size; all callers pass null), the caches' OpenInto(+context, +int).
  Slots: C_Actor 0x60 (int), 0x68 (3 ints), 0x74 SetSeatOccupant(seat, occupant), 0x84 (seat),
  0x90 GetSeatCamera(seat), 0x9C (seat; had 2), 0xAC float(seat) (pi by default; was S_vector),
  0xC4 (seat, bool on), 0xD0 (4 ints); C_HumanController 0x04 (S_vector *), 0x0C (bool completed),
  0x10 (humanId, itemUid, health, removeItem: heals with a medkit), 0x14 (humanId, itemUid),
  0xDC / 0xE0 (const S_HumanCommand &), AI 0x114 (const S_vector *dir); C_ItemContainer 0x54 (int),
  0x5C / 0x60 (int, int); C_BitStream slot 0 is the base Reserve (0x73d2c0, was "IsValid":
  returns BitPosition() <= capacity, `bits` unused; the port returned true, WriteBits ignores it).
  Ghidra only: 25 collision functions, the temp-actor messages, ActorToId / IdToActor, the item
  factories etc. were __cdecl but pop their arguments (now __stdcall; SaveActorItems /
  LoadActorItems and the S_HumanBody anim-length forwarders are __thiscall);
  C_ScriptModule::LoadGame had a 44-argument signature, HandleClientMessage is __fastcall(type, msg),
  the float functions (TimeToKey, SetMusicVolume, ...) returned through a bogus hidden pointer,
  C_Module's stubs, the sound / track / version module slots got their prototypes. False positives
  (by hand): C_Human::Add/Insert/Set/GetOrder are C_SquadController slots past the base vtable,
  GetHandsItem & co. reach C_ItemContainer::GetItem (RET 0), the zz_idioms empty functions, Ghidra
  chunks (PlayTableSound_part2, std_string::CopyTail). (C_EnemyController's 137 vtable slots in
  90_auto.spec ran into the player controller's vtable 0x00811848; fixed in controllers phase 4.)
- ret_audit world.h (2026-10-19): FlashDynamicLight 0x0071ec30 is __fastcall(light ECX, dubbing EDX,
  nearRange, farRange, flag, isActivePlayer), RET 0x10. The port's declaration had no `dubbing`, and
  the sound module's slots 0x50 SetFlashLight / 0x4C SetFlashLightWithNoise took it as `bool on`
  (`TEST EBX,EBX`, then `MOV EDX,EBX` into the call): the number was dropped at every caller. It is
  a dubbing number: the callee loads tables\dabing\%08d.dat and the %08d.wav sample (0x0071ebc0)
  into the slot's sound frame (CreateFrame(4)) and, with `flag`, the quick message (0x0048f180 with
  the sample's length). Callers now pass it whole: SayNow / Say (SpeechCode_Base(code), was `!= 0`),
  the animation note "!a b" (b), C_HumanMovement::SetWeaponFlash 0x00786a70 (`int dubbing` in ECX,
  was `bool on`). Prototypes in names/sound.txt and names/movement.txt (in Ghidra, see below).
  The "dynamic light" / "flash light" names of the table 0x008cb8e0 (16 x 0x44) and these slots are
  wrong: it is the speech (dubbing) table; renamed in the next entry.
- Speech slots rename (2026-10-19, names and comments only): the table 0x008cb8e0 (16 x 0x44) plays
  one dubbing per slot and lip-syncs it: slot +0x00 the tables\dabing\<n>.dat data (8-byte header
  starting with the frame count, then 8-byte frames; frame = play time / 20 ms), +0x04 play time, +0x08 fade timer, +0x0C state (0 free,
  1 attached, 2 playing, 3 closing the mouth over 200 ms, 4 delay, 5 like 2 but its sound is not
  released), +0x14 I3D_sound, +0x18 the speaker's frame, +0x1C its 'SMMR' morph visual (child "a",
  as C_HumanMorph; channels 0..2 are the mouth), +0x20 / +0x24 C_Human +0x2BC / +0x2B8 (only ever
  zeroed in the port), +0x28.. weights, +0x40 a 0..1 ramp; a std::vector of morph fades at
  0x008cb8d4 and a resync flag at 0x008cbd20 (set on unpause). Verified with asm.py dis. Renamed
  (src/ via rename.py, names files, pushed to Ghidra by address): ResetDynamicLights 0x0071e680 ->
