Fixed in this pass: I3D_frame members after m_scale were 4 bytes off (now static_asserted);
I3D_dummy box max corner is at +0x130 (I3D_bbox).

Fixed in the 2026-10-13 pass (all re-applied to Ghidra and re-exported):
- S_quat is {S_vector v; float s;} in memory (identity = 0,0,0,1); files store s first. math.h
  has S_quat::FromFile/ToFile; track keys converted. (Was s-first.)
- ls3d_ghidra.field_size took embedded structs as 4 bytes: every I3D_frame member after m_bound was
  0x2C too far in Ghidra. Sizes now come from the specs.
- 59 auto classes (C_Human, C_Car, C_MeleeTracker, ...) had their recovered layouts under C_auto_<vtable> and a
  partial stub under the chosen name; tools/rename_auto_structs.py moved the stubs aside
  (<name>__stub) and renamed the recovered structs. gen_tier1 keeps the richer of duplicate layouts.
  ALWAYS follow a rename with `apply_members.py --classes <the renamed classes>`: member functions
  keep a `this` type named C_auto_<vtable> that is an orphaned 1-byte placeholder, and their
  decompilation turns into `this[0x188] = (C_X)0x0` byte indexing (2026-10-16: ~420 functions of 38
  classes, +500 tier-1 errors; fixed by re-running apply_members for all of them).
- Typed vtable slots: `apply_ghidra.proto_pointer` emits `ret (*Slot)(args)` WITHOUT `this` and without
  a calling convention (Ghidra's C parser rejects `(__thiscall *)`; the default convention pops the
  stack like thiscall), so calls decompile with the right arguments. `vslot` lines in specs name
  slots whose targets have no usable symbol (e.g. _purecall).
  Naming a slot's target renames the slot in tier 1's (generated) vtable struct, so Ghidra's copy
  must be re-imported or decompiled calls through `vfNN` stop compiling: for spec classes the spec
  import does it; for auto classes run `import_spec.py --structs X_vtbl,...` (2026-10-16: it now
  also generates the 90_auto vtables), then re-export the callers.
- Tier 1 globals (2026-10-16): gen_tier1 declares a data symbol with the structure type its names
  file gives (`g_shotManager @ C_ShotManager`); untyped ones stay uint8_t / uint8_t *.
- C_Module::Unknown28 takes a C_BitStream* (second message hook of the network receive path).
- HUD part 3, the small panels, the pickers, the overlays, the member panels and the signals
  (2026-10-18; `game/interface_status.cpp` (gauges, reticles, weapons, crosshair, stance, progress),
  `interface_selectors.cpp` (order queue, map cursor, weapon selector), `interface_overlays.cpp`
  (damage flash, big message, objective icons, score table, voting, photo flash, explosive timers,
  carnage, pause / countdown texts), `interface_members.cpp`, `interface_signals.cpp`, the shared
  `interface_internal.h`; 0x004a89e0..0x004b0220, 0x004b30e0..0x004b48d0, 0x004bf2e0..0x004c11d0).
  The panels' layouts replaced the opaque storage in `interface.h` and `51_hud.spec` (new types
  C_WeaponSelector_Entry, S_SignalEntry). Text boxes: Ghidra's AddWrappedText / AddFormattedTextId are
  `C_TextBox::SetText` / `SetTextId` (replace run `index`, append past the end).
  * the weapon selector ("select_weapon"): the selected member's hands item (or the empty hands), long
    gun and the belt's weapons and medkits stacked 0.045 down from "select_weapon00", the chosen one
    1.1x with the marker; amounts rounds / ammo carried (launchers count the backpack), items of a kind,
    a medkit's charge % / medkits; [ / ] repeat every 200 ms, closes after 2 s idle, while the member
    holds his breath or has orders; the right button takes the entry in hand (a medkit: HEAL_SELF).
  * the map cursor: 16 shapes (0x0083aed0), the mouse moves it within the screen (0..0.995,
    0..-0.745) or a clip rectangle (0.002 inside); the hovered visual's frame data is its id (member
    panels 100 + slot); the scale eases 5 per second. The order queue: icons "info00..09" by the
    order kinds (state icons 10, 11, 19, 13, 14; actions by the module command), the chosen 1.1x.
  * the member panels ("Soldier%02d"): name (single player rank + last name, NPCs the last name;
    multiplayer the side's rank titles 0x27A6 / 0x27B0), weapon, bars scaled to what is lost (health
    covered at most 95 %, critical icon under 50 %), command / mind icons (state 2 and 3 swap
    places), the photo (death picture x_fc_death.bmp) tinted by the human's +0x1E0 (material slot 4),
    marks: controlled, ear (orders or within 40 m of the active soldier), silenced weapon (item types
    7, 0x16, 0x104, now `items.h` IsSilencedType), medic (first aid over 40), VIP; the visibility "%.0fm".
  * the signals: two tables of 11 pages x 10 entries (single player 0x0083b620, multiplayer
    0x0083b990, embedded); pages 0x32 main, 0x33..0x38 move / combat / target (loud, quiet), 0x39
    formation, 0x3A orders (multiplayer: the team's messages), 0x3B the radio's fire support
    (multiplayer, items 0xF3 / 0xF4); entries enabled by the group (CollectGroup GROUP_NEAR), orders,
    formation, spacing; the target page (BuildTargetPage 0x004bf670, created as a function in Ghidra,
    was only reached by a tail jump) per actor type: doors, radios, supply boxes (ammo or the allied
    list), cars, humans (dead / unconscious / squad / enemy), mines, explosive and document places,
    photo places, wire, meleeTrackers, items...; commands as S_ActionCommand through
    C_Human::PlayerSendActionCommand (target pages: action = the page, command = entry - 100; move /
    combat points from a ray along the view, 40 m for grenades). Keys 1..9, 0, the right button, the wheel.
  Fixed: the order queue and the signals' supply-box items wrote past their 10 icons / entries; the
  score table read 32 player slots whatever the actor count; a missing "AImode*" crashed the member
  panels; the signals' "previous" from nothing chosen walked back through memory; a member's name was

  * the map (the mission's "map" model, "center" at (0.5, -0.375, -1), "map*"'s scale): kind 0 single

    data 1000 + index, MP zones of side -1 by the zone frame's short name) sets the squad state's

  * the inventory (notebook): six slots per page (backpack, head, drop place, hands, uniform, long gun;

    / 250 + line) scrolled by four bars ("scroll13..16", ids 0xF10..0xF40, positions = groups - 3);
    weights "%d/%d kg" and bars, capacities "%s: %d/%d"; the right page (a searched human) or the
    skills (shooting, strength, health, endurance, stealth, first aid, lockpick: health %, strength and
    endurance raw, the rest GetStatFactor %). Drag and drop with a 250 ms double-click window (the
    second click sends the item across, ITEM_CONTAINER_COUNT); dropped off the inventory or on a drop
    place: thrown (not a uniform); on an occupied slot: swapped (RequestTransferItem). Uniforms move
    only with an unconscious body (not +0x1A5). The description after 400 ms on an item: name,
    weight, rounds (behaviours 2..5, 12), muzzle velocity, a scope's zoom tan(40 deg) / tan(fov / 2).
    Only humans are searched (the roll menu offers it on humans alone), so the right side is a human.
  * the scroll bars: arrows (normal / actived / inactived looks) and a slider moving along

  * the roll menu: up to 9 numbered actions + cancel on the actor under the crosshair within 1.8 m (or

- Known Ghidra artifact: ESP-frame functions that call LS3D vtable methods (e.g. driver->CreateFrame)
  are tracked as if the callee did not pop its arguments, so later parameters show as unaff_* /
  shifted. Read those from the disassembly (`gstr.py d ADDR N`) when porting.

MeleeTracker (2026-10-19, in progress): the meleeTracker spans 0x005c0ce0..0x005d86b0 (~93 KB) and is being ported in
three parts — 1 the body (C_MeleeTrackerMovement at C_MeleeTracker +0x90 with the embedded C_MeleeTrackerAnimMixer at +0xA0),
2 the AI controllers (five vtables) and path planning (0x005c9050..), 3 the C_MeleeTracker actor. The body
is itself the largest module, so it is split: part 1a (the animation mixer) is done, part 1b (the
movement physics) is next.
  Part 1a — C_MeleeTrackerAnimMixer (game/melee_tracker_movement.h, game/melee_tracker_anim_mixer.cpp; 0x005c6d10..0x005c7b00 and
  the serialization 0x005cfd20 / 0x005d0180 / 0x005d0950, 17 functions). Two tracks of two stages
  cross-fade on the model's first four animation stages (the rest tick at the plain movement time);
  the root-motion speed blends old->new by the blend progress and, with the add-move and slide, gives
  the move of the body; "a1"/"a2"/"m" note keys add overlay events, "s" a footstep. Modelled on the
  human mixer. Compiles clean (MSVC 19.51) and ret_audit is clean. Fixes: the note callback skips a
  model with no actor (the original reaches the mixer as actor + 0xA0 through a near-null pointer); at
  most three note events a track an update (the original writes past the array). Quirk kept: the move's
  w and the loaded velocity vectors' w are left as scratch (the original reads uninitialised stack).
  The Ghidra names and the struct types (C_MeleeTrackerAnimMixer, C_MeleeTrackerMovement, C_MeleeTracker) will be imported in one
  pass once part 1b lands, to avoid importing the meleeTracker's embedded types twice; ported.txt lists the 17
  mixer addresses.
  Part 1b (C_MeleeTrackerMovement, game/melee_tracker_movement.cpp) — the movement machinery is done: the full
  byte-exact 0x410 layout (C_MeleeTracker +0x90..+0x4A0; back-pointer at +0x160, collision spheres/volumes at
  +0x190..+0x1F0, the current anim descriptor S_MeleeTrackerAnimSequence at +0x284 and a 5-deep queue at +0x2A8),
  Init (0x005c7d30), Reset (0x005d1f50), Save/Load (0x005d09b0 / 0x005d11a0), SetOptimized(Controller),
  the mode/sub-mode priority system (0x005c8490/8540/8570) and the anim-sequence ring (0x005c4b90/4c30/
  4c70), the net position + interpolation (0x005c0f50 SetNetPosition, 0x005c0d10, 0x005c0d60,
  0x005c1430 ApplyNetInterp), facing/turning (0x005c1e60 SetFacing, 0x005c1b80 TurnToFacing,
  0x005c89c0 TurnDir), the per-frame animation update (0x005c18c0) and events/footsteps (0x005c1600),
  CheckGround (0x005c88b0), the Bite (0x005c49a0) and the Update orchestration (0x005c8b30). ~30
  functions, compiles clean (MSVC) and ret_audit is clean. Dropped: the copy protection makes a
  cracked copy's bite do 10x damage (the port always uses the normal damage). Quirks kept: LoadGame
  reads only 4 bytes of the 0xB4-byte anim queue; QueuePopBack sets m_35c (not m_360) to 4 on
  underflow. Fixes while porting (caught by ret_audit/review): TurnToFacing and TurnDir work on the
  actor direction (C_MeleeTracker +0x40), not the frame matrix, and TurnToFacing's close case snaps straight to
  the facing; both turn helpers were mis-ported at first. TurnToFacing has an unused second argument
  (the caller passes 1); ApplyNetInterp takes the frame time as an argument (= m_388) but reads it from
  the field.
  Part 1b Move collision core — done: Move (0x005c3420, the sphere-stepping move with gravity,
  step-climb retry and the ground ray tests via g_collisionWorld.MoveSpheresStepping / CollisionRayTest),
  ContactResponse (0x005c2260, gravity landing), AlignToSlope (0x005c26d0, tilts the body to the ground
  slope), SlopeSlide (0x005c43d0, blends the step end toward the ground and sets the body rate), and the
  helpers MeleeTrackerAnimClass (0x005d86f0), gravity 0x005c62c0, slide-step 0x005c6250, VolumeMaterial 0x005c6360,
  IsGrounded 0x005c6380. Compiles clean (MSVC) and ret_audit is clean. The x87 gravity/slide maths and
  the slope alignment are faithful transcriptions (dimensional quirks kept). Fixes/decisions: Move builds
  a "moving" flag and a surface normal from an uninitialised stack vector (local_270, read but never
  written in the shipped code — a latent bug); the port zeroes them (deterministic; the slope-slide via
  that path stays off) and documents it. SlopeSlide's original param_2 bundles the normal + hit volume
  (+0x14) + moving flag (+0x11) as one pointer aliasing that stack region; the port passes them as
  separate arguments (so its arg count differs from the original's — ret_audit's address was dropped from
  the decl for that reason). The gravity-distance helper (0x005c62c0) returns a dimensionally-odd value
  but is transcribed exactly from the x87.
  Part 1b animation-selection cluster — done, finishing C_MeleeTrackerMovement: BuildSequence (0x005c5030,
  the mode/sub-mode state machine that pushes S_MeleeTrackerAnimSequence transition runs onto the 5-deep queue),
  StepAnimation (0x005c4cb0, plays the next queued animation via the mixer and g_meleeTrackerAnims),
  CheckAttackSequence (0x005c5a90, AI-driven), PlayAnimSound (0x005c5bf0, the per-animation barks/
  growls/whines from the mouth frame), MeleeTrackerBarkTurn (0x005c6c70) and the helpers MeleeTrackerAnimOneShot
  (0x005c8880). g_meleeTrackerAnims[0x17] (Tables\dog_anim.sav) is declared in meleeTracker_movement.h and defined in the
  cpp (loaded at runtime by the actor). Compiles clean (MSVC) and ret_audit is clean. StepAnimation has
  an unused trailing stack argument the caller passes.

  THE MELEE_TRACKER BODY (part 1) IS DONE: C_MeleeTrackerAnimMixer (game/melee_tracker_anim_mixer.cpp, 17 functions) and

  0 errors/0 warnings), ret_audit clean. Ghidra: the struct types are in types/52_melee_tracker.spec and were
  imported (import_spec.py 52_melee_tracker.spec; 7 meleeTracker structs + externals, no _func_N, externals intact); the
  function names/prototypes are in names/melee_tracker.txt and were applied (apply_ghidra --symbols). ported.txt
  lists the 61 body addresses. Unreferenced -> tier 1: 0x005c1ec0, 0x005c31f0, 0x005c63f0, 0x005c64e0,
  and the CRT/RTTI atexit stubs (0x005c0ce0, 0x005c1b50, 0x005c4b60, 0x005c6220, 0x005c6ce0, 0x005c7d00,
  0x005cfcf0, 0x005d0980, 0x005d1fd0).
  Part 2 (the meleeTracker AI, in progress) — the AI is a polymorphic controller hierarchy: a base controller
  C_auto_00811fd0 (0xC0 bytes, vtable 0x00811fd0 -- meleeTracker at +0x10, the controller mode at +0x14, the
  orders bitfield at +0x20 [MELEE_TRACKER_ORDER_* in meleeTracker_ai.h], a checkpoint path std::vector<int> at +0x78, the
  current target at +0x88, destination positions at +0x90/+0xA0) and subclasses for the behaviours; the
  scripted meleeTracker uses vtable 0x00812250 and adds the script hook (+0xC0) and a bark timer (+0xC4), giving
  C_MeleeTrackerAI = 0xC8. Other controllers: C_auto_00812048 (0x60), C_auto_008120c0/008120c4 (0x14), plus the
  vtables 0x008121e0, 0x00812340, 0x008122c8. An A* pathfinder on the checkpoint network lives at
  0x005c9050..0x005cb090 (FindPath 0x005cb500 -> the search 0x005cb610). ~26 KB total.
    Part 2a — C_MeleeTrackerAI layout + the script-command API (C_MeleeTrackerScript::ExecuteCommand), done:
    game/melee_tracker_ai.h (the controller layout + the MELEE_TRACKER_ORDER_* bitfield) and game/melee_tracker_ai.cpp (MoveTo,

    HearRange, SetOwner, SetBarking, ClearAlarm = 0x005cf700..0x005cfbb0). The thin C_MeleeTrackerAI was moved
    out of world.h (world.h now includes game/melee_tracker_ai.h); C_Script gained `friend class C_MeleeTrackerAI` (MoveTo
    advances the script when no path is found). Compiles clean (full tree 127 files, 0/0) and ret_audit
    is clean. SetScript (vtable +0x40, address not yet read) is a provisional `m_script = script`.
    Part 2b-i — the AI update pipeline + attack/owner management + behaviour apply, done
    (game/melee_tracker_ai.cpp): Update (0x005ce190, the per-frame dispatch: UpdateTimers -> clear the behaviour
    buffers -> CullSightings -> DecideTarget -> DecideMove -> FollowPath -> SubUpdate -> Apply -> set
    the facing on the movement), UpdateTimers (0x005cdf90), SubUpdate (0x005ce110, the base stance
    weights from the orders flags), Apply (0x005cec60, commits the chosen behaviour weights to the
    movement via SetMode / SetSubModeA/B / MeleeTrackerBarkTurn), SetMeleeTracker (0x005ce630), ResetState (0x005ce650,
    copies the transform + owner from the previous controller on a swap), Disable (0x005ce6e0),
    SetAlarmBase (0x005ce700), BeginAttack (0x005cd0a0), ClearAttack (0x005cd120), OwnerPosition
    (0x005cd690), SetOwnerActor (0x005cd720). The behaviour-weight buffers are two shared float[0x40]
    globals (g_meleeTrackerActionA/B at 0x008AC820/0x008AC720). From C_MeleeTracker::FUN_005d5890 (part 3): the actor
    swaps the controller by mode -- mode 5 = the scripted C_MeleeTrackerAI (vtable 0x00812250, 0xD0 bytes);
    mode 1 = vtable 0x00812340 (0x4B0 bytes, the full roam/combat controller); mode 4 = vtable
    0x008122c8 (0xC0); mode 7 = vtable 0x008121e0 (0x270). C_MeleeTrackerAI was corrected to 0xD0 bytes and +0x18
    is the owner actor (not an enabled flag). C_MeleeTrackerMovement::GetSeqAnimA/B/C were made public for Apply.
    ret_audit clean (24 decls), full tree compiles (127 files, 0/0). SubUpdate takes the frame time as an
    argument it ignores.
    Part 2b-ii-a — the self-contained sensing/steering helpers, done (game/melee_tracker_ai.cpp): SetMoveToward
    (0x005cc5f0, sets the desired facing m_facing toward a point, horizontal, with the AI's axis-snap
    normalise), BarkAtSound (0x005cddd0, barks + raises the alarm bit 0x80000 when a heard sound's
    loudness field +0x1c > 9 and the bark cooldown m_0bc has elapsed; m_0bc = rand()%300 + 800),
    BarkIdle (0x005cde20, the same bark on an idle tick; __thiscall with an unread stack arg, RET 4),
    OnSightTarget (0x005cdf30, a sensed target: clears the chase and arms a re-acquire delay m_0b4 when
    the alarm bit was set, and (defensive, order bit 0x200) latches a seen human as m_orderedTarget +
    bit 0x400). ret_audit clean (29 decls), meleeTracker_ai.cpp compiles 0/0.
    Part 2c — the checkpoint path, done (game/melee_tracker_ai.cpp). The graph A* itself is
    C_CheckpointNetwork::FindMeleeTrackerPath (0x00522470, already ported in checkpoint_search.cpp); 2c is the
    meleeTracker-side path building on top of it. FindPath (0x005cb500: leads the start 0.2 ahead of the meleeTracker
    when running, resolves the checkpoints nearest the ends via FindNearest, calls the search),
    RunPathSearch (0x005cb610: FindMeleeTrackerPath -> ClearPath -> build a waypoint per checkpoint ->
    SmoothPath -> snap the last to the goal -> drop the first if the meleeTracker is already within its radius
    or it is behind, unless stuck), SmoothPath (0x005cb9b0, 1.2 KB: projects each point onto the line
    between its neighbours, clamping to the point's radius -- cuts corners), ClearPath (0x005cbe60:
    frees the waypoints, clears the path/order bits, advances a waiting scripted MoveTo), ResetPath
    (0x005cb410, vtable +0x0c). The meleeTracker reuses the human AI's waypoint node S_AIWaypoint (0x30) from
    its OWN pool (0x008ac920), modelled as C_ObjectPool<S_AIWaypoint>{16}; AllocMeleeTrackerWaypoint (0x005ce4c0,
    scales the checkpoint's 1/16-m radius byte) / ReleaseMeleeTrackerWaypoint (0x005cafc0). MeleeTrackerPathLookahead
    (0x005c8b10, a float-nonzero idiom on the movement's m_24c). The controller path vector is a raw
    {lead +0x74, begin +0x78, end +0x7c, cap +0x80} of S_AIWaypoint*; PathPushBack/FreeAllWaypoints
    reproduce the original insert/clear. Fixes: the original left the smoothed points' w and each
    node's w uninitialised (kept the waypoint's / set 1) and would deref an empty path in the trim
    (guarded). ret_audit clean (33 decls), full tree compiles (127 files, 0/0).
    Still subsumed/pending from 2c: the pool's composite ctor/dtor (0x005ce2f0/0x005ce400, build BOTH
    the 0x30 node pool and a co-located 0x20-byte pool for the path-following geometry) and the generic
    array helpers (0x005cadd0 insert, 0x005caf70 erase, 0x005cadb0/0x005cb030/0x005cb060/0x005cb090) are
    reproduced for the meleeTracker path but finish with the geometry cluster below.
    Part 2b-ii-b (partial) — DistanceToTarget (0x005cde60, vtable +0x28) done: FLT_MAX with no target,

  Part 3a-i (C_MeleeTracker layout) DONE (2026-10-19, game/melee_tracker.h): C_MeleeTracker : public C_Actor (vtable 0x008120e0)
  with the embedded body (C_MeleeTrackerMovement m_movement at the original +0x90), the AI ptr (m_ai +0x534),
  the sighting list (m_sightingsBegin/End/Cap +0x564/0x568/0x56c), the second list (m_574/m_578), the
  steering fields (m_580/m_584/m_588 vector, m_590.. position, m_5a0 time) and the save fields
  (m_4a0.. counters, vectors m_4c0/m_4f0/m_510, etc.). world.h's thin C_MeleeTracker stub replaced by
  #include "game/melee_tracker.h"; the stub's accessors (GetAI/GetBodyFrame/GetSightId/IsAlive/GetAlarmType)
  are now inline. meleeTracker_ai.cpp's MeleeTrackerMovement(meleeTracker) helper fixed from the +0x90 placeholder to
  &meleeTracker->GetMovement(). Full tree compiles (127 files, 0/0).
  KEY FINDING: the ported actors are NOT byte-exact to the binary. The engine's S_vector4 is 16-byte
  aligned; the port's is 4-byte, so C_Actor compiles to 0x70 (not the binary's 0x90) and every derived
  actor (C_Human, now C_MeleeTracker) is compressed. The `+0xNNN` comments are the ORIGINAL offsets; the code
  accesses everything by NAME (this is why C_MeleeTracker can't use offsetof/sizeof static_asserts, unlike the
  standalone byte-exact C_MeleeTrackerMovement). Save compatibility holds because save/load is field-by-field
  through the chunk API, and raw-dumped blocks stay internally contiguous (m_4a0[4 ints], m_4b0[4]).
  CONSEQUENCE for 3b: C_MeleeTracker::SaveGame raw-writes each sighting's 0x20 bytes, so S_MeleeTrackerSighting MUST be
  built byte-exact (0x20), like S_AIWaypoint. The decision core (2b-ii-b) will access C_MeleeTracker fields by
  name (meleeTracker->m_sightingsBegin, meleeTracker->m_580, ...), not by offset.

  SaveGame/LoadGame DEFERRED: traced C_MeleeTracker::SaveGame's chunk nesting (0x385 > 0x2a9 {0{m_4a0[16],
  m_4b0[16]}, leaves 1..0x12, 0x13{per-sighting subchunk + actor-id leaf}, 0x14{second list}} +

  So the order is: 3b (sighting struct + subsystem) FIRST, then the controller save (0x5d2dd0/

  3b-ii LAST PRODUCER -- the sense/sound handler 0x005d6000, FULLY ANALYSED (2026-10-19), port
  pending careful transcription (the single hardest meleeTracker function: two nested jump-table switches +
  x87 loudness accumulation). Model: a sense event is a float[8] {pos +0..0x0c, listener FRAME +0x10
  (its m_data +0xec -> actor = the meleeTracker), loudness 1-distSq/rangeSq +0x14, group +0x18, source actor
  +0x1c}, built and passed by the scan 0x005d7760 (called from Tick 0x5d5620; it caches the humans at
  meleeTracker+0x54c/0x550 and, every >999 ms per meleeTracker->m_55c, emits an event per live human within hearing
  range m_4ac). 5d6000: bail if AI suspended or mode==1; require source is a targetable human
  (type 3, role not 2/3) within m_4ac; a jump-table switch on the group sets a per-group WEIGHT in a
  stack slot (constants 1/2/3/10/12/14/15/16; group 0 sub-switches on C_Human::GetMoveSpeedClass;
  group 4 raises an immediate alarm when loudness*3 > 0.95 via the AI +0x44 hook; groups 5/7 skip),
  then finds the sighting matching (source==+0x14, group==+0x10) and accumulates
  loudness = (int)((float)oldLoudness + weight) + time = QPC-us-low, or AllocMeleeTrackerSightings a new one
  (time = timeGetTime()) + pushes it to meleeTracker+0x564; finally OnHeardSound (alarms once loudness > 10).
  The weights live in the jump table at the case blocks 0x5d6168/6172/617c/6186/6190/619a/61a4/61d8;
  the two jump tables (group, and move-speed-class) must be read out exactly before porting. The scan
  0x5d7760 and its human cache (the 0x0045ca80 insert into meleeTracker+0x54c) go with it. NOTE: vision already
  works (OnSightEvent), so the meleeTracker senses without 5d6000; C_MeleeTracker save/load does not depend on it.
  Part 3a-ii controller save/load DONE (game/melee_tracker_ai.cpp): C_MeleeTrackerAI::SaveGame (0x005d2dd0 scripted +
  0x005d2000 base) / LoadGame (0x005d2fc0 + 0x005d21d0). The base writes chunk 0x22b8 { 0: owner id
  (m_owner->GetNetId() or -1), 1: m_alarm, 2: m_flags, 3: m_savedPos xyz, 4: m_facing xyz, 5: m_050
  xyz }; the scripted meleeTracker adds 0x2054 { 0: m_barkTimer }. Load mirrors it (owner via
  g_actorsModule.GetActor(id)); Fix: the vectors read 0xc (xyz) so w is kept (the original stored a
  4th uninitialised word). ret_audit clean (44 decls), 128 files 0/0.

  CORRECTION (3c-ii): the asm stores every scalar with a raw MOV into a 4-byte slot -- the
  decompiler's "(float)field" casts were artifacts of the float-typed slot, not FILD conversions. So
  the bytes (3/4/8/9/0x11) are saved zero-extended as int 0/1 (not 0.0f/1.0f), and the scalars keep
  their real types: int m_50c/m_520 (behaviour)/m_524/m_528/m_52c/m_55c (scan timer), float
  m_500 (an angle)/m_508. The first pass had wrongly retyped m_50c..m_52c to float and written the
  flags as floats; both fixed, the save format is now byte-identical.

  raw 0x20, re-resolves source by id, pushes via PushSighting = the 0x005d47a0 insert; 0x14 rebuilds the
  seen list, dropping null entries), then m_movement.LoadGame, then chunk 0 { leaf 1 = mode ->
  SetController(mode) -> m_ai->LoadGame }, then SetOptimized(m_optimized) and, when dead, leaves the
  sight module (observer + target) and removes the sound listener. ret_audit clean, 128 files 0/0.
  RENAME FALLOUT FIXED: the 15 script keywords in src/script/script_tables.cpp (DOG_Move, DOG_Attack,
  DOG_SETMODE_*, DOG_SETAIMODE_*, DOG_HafHaf, ...) had been rewritten by the MeleeTracker rename; they
  are game data matched against the shipped .scr files and were restored verbatim (all 15 confirmed in
  the binary's .data/.rdata strings).
  Field notes (for reference)
  for the write: chunk 0 raw-writes m_4a0[0x10] then m_4b0[0x10]; chunk 1/5/0xc are S_vectors (0xc,
  keep the 4th word -- original reads garbage into m_4cc/m_4fc/m_51c); chunk 2 stores
  {m_4dc,m_4d0,m_4d4,m_4d8} (0x10) and unpacks the same; chunks 3/4/8/9/0x11 are bytes saved
  zero-extended as int 0/1 and loaded as (int != 0) -> m_4e8/m_4e9/m_504/m_505/m_optimized (save has no
  chunk 7; load case 7 is dead); chunks 6/0xa/0xb/0xd/0xe/0xf/0x10/0x12 are raw 4-byte copies of
  m_500 (float)/m_508 (float)/m_50c/m_520/m_524/m_528/m_52c/m_55c (ints) -- see the CORRECTION above;
  chunk 0x13 = the sightings (per-entry subchunk i { raw 0x20 } + a leaf (i+100 if source else
  i+1000) = source->GetNetId() or -1); chunk 0x14 = the seen-enemy list (leaf i = actor id); then
  m_movement.SaveGame, then chunk 0 { leaf 1 = m_ai->GetMode(), then m_ai->SaveGame }. LoadGame must
  recreate the controller from the saved mode before m_ai->LoadGame; read the 0x13/0x14 loaders
  (0x5d3a50) still to be studied.

  0x005d7760), TrackVelocity (0x005d64e0: m_velocity = (pos - m_lastPos) * 1000 / m_lastFrameTime,
  quirk kept: infinite for one frame if the actor moved before the first measurement),
  SetLookBehaviour (0x005d8070: state = request & ~0x11, bit 4 = fast; 2/6 start a look at the
  target (speed rand%30/100 + 0.6 or 1.0), 4 = turn back ahead, 8 = look around `param` times; the
  return value is unused by both callers -> void), UpdateLookBehaviour (0x005d82b0, flow checked
  against the asm + its jump table 0x5d86ac: the look-around timer per repeat count 0..3, a new point
  20 m out at the facing turned +-(0.22 + k/11) rad, the "reached" dwell re-armed from RandFloat()*1000;
  always ends by copying m_lookTarget to m_lookPoint except on the early exit). The look-around is a
  procedural HEAD LOOK (cosmetic): HeadLook 0x005d7a10 (declared, body pending) turns the head bone
  m_headFrame toward m_lookPoint with a log-shaped rate by m_lookRateMode scaled by m_lookSpeed,
  yaw m_headYaw clamped to +-0.97 rad, through m_headController (an LS3D object made in the frame
  setup by driver slots 0x78/0x74 and attached with frame vtable +0xB4; it is driven via
  (*(m_headController+8))->vtable +0x1C set direction / +0x08 release) -- port it with the setup.
  Fields named: m_headController/m_headFrame/m_headTurning/m_lookActive/m_lookPoint/m_headYaw/

  Uses math.h's S_vector::Normalize/Magnitude (the same SDK inlines the earlier NormalizeXYZ /
  SafeLength copies in melee_tracker_ai.cpp duplicate -- tidy later). m_ai uses in Tick are
  null-guarded only while part 2d is unported. ret_audit clean (28 decls), 128 files 0/0.

  Part 3b DONE (hearing, game/melee_tracker.cpp): OnSoundHeard (static stdcall listener callback

  the controller's +0x48 (scripted: OnHeardSound, alarms over 10; base 0x811fd0: BarkAtSound, now

  Tick): every 1000 ms (m_55c), refills the players list from the 32 player slots if empty, then each
  live player within hearRange is passed to OnSoundHeard as a footstep (chapter 0, intensity

  Part 2b-ii-b DONE (the decision core, game/melee_tracker_ai.cpp; every flow checked against the asm
  since the decomp drops blocks): DecideTarget (0x005cd1f0: holdBack = DEFENSIVE unless HAS_TARGET
  with the ordered target; m_0b4 += dt (< 100000); no target -> mode 4 attacks anything seen/heard
  after 1.2 s; attacking (0x20000): dead / in-vehicle target -> ClearAttack + CheckAttackSequence; the
  target's position 0.1 s ahead (m_velocity +0x1B0); bite range 4.2 held back else 2.1 on the run
  (anim class 1) / 1.3; out of it: FindTarget(false) unless within lunge reach with a short path
  (then 0x10004); in it: bite (actionB[6], not held back) unless anim class 2, then 0x4004 unless

  timer; then face the target), FindTarget (0x005cc8d0) + ScoreTarget (0x005cc7a0: QUIRK kept, the

  human.h), CheckReached (0x005cccc0, RET 4: arg unread), DecideMove (0x005cd890, RET 4: heel at
  OwnerPosition, margin 0.2 / 1.2 behind / (1 - dot*1.17) + 0.2 off-axis, run beyond margin+5 else walk
  speed 1.4/1.2/1.1/1.0; beyond 3.5 m or on a path: 0x4 and the path leads; hysteresis start beyond
  margin+0.2, stop within margin, then face the owner's way), FollowPath (0x005cbf00, RET 4: walk
  angle limit (a + 0.5) * pi/3 with a = min(2, d or d/4 running), turning on the spot (0x20000000)

  Part 2d DONE (the controller hierarchy, game/melee_tracker_ai.h/.cpp): the six vtables diffed
  slot by slot (each vtable is followed by the next one's RTTI slot, 0 here). The original is a
  real class tree (the full AI's dtor 0x005d5b50 resets the vtable to 0x00812048):
    C_MeleeTrackerController 0x00812048 (root, 0x70: tracker, mode, owner, alarm, orders, transform,
      the two behaviour-buffer pointers +0x60/+0x64, frame counter +0x68; 27 slots 0x00..0x68 --
      Attach/Reset/dtor/Detach/ResetState/FrameUpdate(+0x14)/Tick/TestSight/+0x20/GetTarget/
      DistanceToTarget (FLT_MAX)/TakeChanged/SetOwnerActor/GetOwner/SetAlarm/ReleaseScript/SetScript/
      OnBulletHit/OnHeardSound/OnSpottedEnemy/OnLostEnemy/OnSeenHuman (Hit's hook)/OnNetState(+0x58)/
      OnNetControl(+0x5C)/PrepareSave/SaveGame/LoadGame; root save/load = chunk 0x22b8; Apply,
      Disable, SetMeleeTracker, Reset 0x005ce5d0, ResetState, TestSight are root code)
    C_MeleeTrackerAI 0x00811fd0 (the full AI, 0xC0, abstract: +0x6C SubUpdate, +0x70
      DistanceToOwnerSpot; its own Reset is InitBase, Detach ResetPath; its hooks are the old
      BarkAtSound (+0x48), the spotted bark (+0x4C, was BarkIdle), OnSightTarget (+0x54); its
      save/load 0x005d25b0 / 0x005d28b0 unreachable, not ported)

        base reads the script via ScriptOf() = mode 5 only, which also fixes MoveTo's unguarded
        +0xC0 read on another variant)

        hearing / spotted hooks)
    C_MeleeTrackerRemote 0x008121e0 (mode 7, 0x270, a client's mirror: own buffers +0x70/+0x170,
      FrameUpdate applies them and clears B; OnNetState reads ReadControlState -- QUIRK kept: the

      local game: Tick points the buffers at g_controls' held / pressed values (the original

  C_MeleeTracker: m_ai is a C_MeleeTrackerController*; SetController builds all four variants;
  Update now calls the controller's FrameUpdate (+0x14) as the original does (3c-ii had left it
  out as empty for the scripted controller); GetFullAI() / GetScriptedAI() (by mode) for the
  script commands (ClearAlarm -> SetAlarm via +0x38; HafHaf needs the scripted one; fix: commands
  skipped without a full AI); OnSightEvent's lost path calls OnLostEnemy; OnSoundHeard calls
  OnBulletHit. The caller of the +0x58/+0x5C net hooks is not identified (the tracker's own
  OnMessage/Unknown20 are empty). ret_audit clean on the tracker headers (a pre-existing finding
  in world.h FlashDynamicLight 0x0071ec30: 5 args vs RET 0x10; fixed 2026-10-19, see the RET-n
  audit entries).

    entries left script.txt; the other modules' files renamed (TestSightOfMeleeTracker,
    FindMeleeTrackerPath, IsMeleeTrackerPassable, ProcessMeleeTrackerAlerts, FindMeleeTrackerTarget,

    StepAnimation is __thiscall with a stack argument, 0x005d5c70 is C_MeleeTracker::PrintText

  - 52_melee_tracker.spec: + S_MeleeTrackerSighting, S_MeleeTrackerStats and the controller tree

  CREATOR PORTED (2026-10-19): C_MeleeTrackerCreator (melee_tracker.h/.cpp; Create 0x005d4a60,
  GetName 0x005d4ad0, Destroy 0x005d4ae0, object 0x008ac950) registers itself in
  g_actorCreators[ACTOR_MELEE_TRACKER] like C_HumanCreator (static initialiser 0x005d4a40; its thunk
  0x005d4a30 and Create's EH free 0x005d4ac0 absorbed), so missions can spawn trackers.
  GetName returns the original's "Dog" (game data; nothing in the binary reads it: g_actorCreators
  is only used for Create/Destroy), and actor.h's type list says so. Fix: g_actorCreators
  (actor.cpp) was a null pointer in the port, so the creators' static registrations wrote through
  null at startup; it now points at a constant-initialised table (the original's 0x00839460 is
  pre-set to 0x0089b530 and never written).

(C_RigidBody 0x4c84a0.., vtable 0x810700).

    order without padding, the per-axle arrays (12 axles x {22 floats, 3 flags, 5 floats, 1 flag},

  - NaN-exact conditions from the disassembly: _re/tools/x87cmps.py classified all 304 x87
    compares (FNSTSW + TEST/AND mask + Jcc -> the set of outcomes {lt, eq, gt, unordered} that
    jump); the decompile's operand can be wrong when the value stays on the FPU stack, its
    comparison text is not NaN-exact. Most checks let a NaN through as valid.

    frames[n - 1]; a group's first visual is the part (keeping its own mesh). SetupParts moves the

  - Pedals: above 1 is 1, below 0.05 is 0; no throttle with the engine off. Mode +0x550 (ApplyParams
    sets 0 or 2): 1 refuses the brake and reverse and sends the throttle to +0x6FC or +0x700 by the
    second argument. With +0x229D (on after Setup; inferred an automatic gearbox) in reverse (+0x874 <
    0) the throttle brakes and the brake drives. The brake and the handbrake ease in: every call adds
    +0x7C4 to their time (+0x724 / +0x710, at most +0x720 / +0x70C) and scales by the fraction reached
    (+0x229B: the brake at once). Steering: v((1 - v)k + v) with k = +0x5F4 (0.5 when made), mirrored
    for the left, x +0x934 into +0x948; SetUnknown4f23b0 uses the same curve with +0x5F8 when +0x229C
    (the player passes 1 - an input axis, a dead driver 0: inferred the clutch). SetGear(-1..count)
    refuses a gear whose revs (new ratio / current ratio x +0x864) would pass the rev limit, and with
    +0xF6C bit 0x4000 does nothing until +0x63C > 0. All return true except as noted (were void).
  - SetEngine(on, instant): the second argument was named `sound`; it means at once: on/off directly
    (off: revs 0, the engine sounds stopped). Otherwise starting needs more than 0.02 l of fuel (and
    +0x610 > 0 with bit 0x4000) or plays the failed start (0x0051a620, sounds on), then 0x00518710
    switches through the engine sounds (part 1d).
  - ApplyImpulse(impulse, point, flags, factors): bit 0 skips the push (velocity +0x410 += impulse /

  - Lifetime: every body is in g_rigidBodies (0x0089bcd4; the ctor appends, the dtor moves the last into
    its place); ForgetActor 0x004cff70 asks each body from the list's end (~C_Actor calls it).
  - The hierarchy box (GetHierarchyBox 0x004c7db0 / AddHierarchyBox 0x004c7e50, a static I3D_bbox
    0x0089bcf0): the frame's own visual box (else the inverted 1e16 box, its w kept) and its visual
    descendants' 8 corners through their local matrices; a non-visual frame ends the descent, a visual
    with an empty box adds nothing but its children still count.
  - SetFrame: the place, matrices and axes; at rest, set directly. Setup (from the hierarchy box): the
    centre of mass its middle; the inverse inertia of the box 5 cm larger each way, a cube's (side the
    average) when its three ratios are within 0.5..2, else the longest side's on the two other axes and
    that times (longest / middle)^2 on its own; the skin half the smallest side, at most 0.04; the 8
    corners and 6 face middles; gravity (0, -m g, 0); bounce 1.01, contact range 0.04, stiffness g m
    12.5, retest square 4 m; at rest when slower than 1e-5 squared. SetupWheel: inertia 1 / (r^2 m / 2)
    on each axis, range 0.03, bounce 1.05, stiffness g m 37.03704, the volume moving with it.
  - Start: into the collision world (the box as the volume, a sphere to its farther corner, type 0x801 /
    0x80802), the timers and records cleared, at rest unless it moves at 0.1 m/s; Stop; SetState (coming
    to rest from moving tells the callback, moving clears the slow time, at rest or fallen a body with
    params +0x41 gets the doubled box as its volume); Nudge; Wake.
  - Impulses: ApplyImpulse (x 1.1, about the centre of mass through the inverse inertia in the body's
    frame; with a hit material the frame's hardest is kept for the hit sound) and AddImpulse (as given, no
    hit record); both wake the body.
  - Step: nothing when slow past the rest delay or still over 2 s; frames over 83 ms cut to 83 and the
    time scaled (m_timeScale); longer than m_maxStep (0.028 s), equal substeps. Tick: Step; the slow time
    (under 0.3 m/s and 0.1 rad/s) and the still time (within 0.2 m); slow past the 0.6 s rest delay or
    still over 2 s: rest through Topple, or with actors under it the last record whose actor moved over
    4 mm wakes it; else the records not touched for a second are dropped; under -85 m/s: fell; the frame
    updated, its sector and the volume moved.
  - Topple: the frame's axes as directions from the centre of mass (normalised, turned upward), the
    strictly most upright one the height; over 2.5 times one of the others (x: y then z; y: x, z; z: x, y;
    the base the first unless only the second is short): pushed at 0.12 m/s along the two others' sum,
    at the centre raised by the first corner's distance x the ratio. Ties rest.
  - Saved games: a 0xA4 record (version 5): place, rotation, the constants, speed, spin, the disc's state,
    the hit material.
  - Checked with asm.py: ApplyImpulse pops 6 arguments and reads 2, Wake pops 8 and reads none (kept in

  - Integrate 0x004cc520: the spin x dt in the body's axes (the inverse matrix without its move), as
    quaternions about right, up and dir composed in turn (the inlined product: s = s1 s2 - v1.v2
    summed z, y, x; v = (s1 v2 + s2 v1) + v1 x v2); dir and up turned; with a centre of mass the
    place turned about it (T(-c) R T(c); the original's first matrix has an unset w, which LS3D's
    affine product never reads); right = up x dir, up = dir x right, each normalised; the move added;
    the frame placed by slot 0x10, now named SetWorldPosDir3 (LS3DF 0x1001cca0: world pos, dir, up).
  - Collide 0x004cccc0 (RET 0x1C, its dt and 7th argument unread): the point's velocity (the body's
    less spin x arm); moving into the normal, the impulse -bounce x approach / (n.((I^-1 (r x n)) x r)
    + 1/m) (the denominator at least 1e-6, compared as a double), into velocity and spin; the frame's
    hardest impulse kept with its point; friction along ((slide x n) x n), the impulse x friction x
    sin(the slide's angle to n) (FSIN's unrounded value), at most 3 x the weight's.
  - FilterContact 0x004ce990: the callback (ECX 1, EDX the body, the context and a contact record;
    EAX 1 refuses); tent canvas (0x43) and paper (0x55) always count; water (0x1F, 0x20, 0x46) never;
    an actor's volume (flag 0x40 and an actor) not with m_ignoreActors, else `onActor` set for a car
    or an animation actor, cleared for others (untouched for other volumes).
  - StepBox 0x004cb2b0: gravity, the move (Integrate), the centre again; under 1 m/s every point is
    tested and the spin damped (1 - dt / 2); else the points ahead of the motion, and every other step
    (m_testTrailing, was m_unknown5F9) the ones behind. Each tested point (13 down to 0): a ray from
    the centre before the move to 0.3 m past it (types 0x88002, flags 0x200000) when it moved or an
    actor's record names it or m_contactsChanged; else the planes the points met last (the first,
    from 13 down, it is in). Water (flag 0x10000): g 9, over 1 m/s x 0.75, effect 0x2D 2 cm over the
    hit, the ray again without water. A hit the filter keeps sets the point's plane, surface (the
    material), actor; a point in its surface gets Collide (when moving into it) and its actor's record
    (renewed to 1000 ms, or appended, the array growing by m_actorContactsGrowBy); the deepest point

    over 0.5 m/s slows by 200 m/s per second down to 0.5; the frame placed (SetPos, Update), the
    matrices and the centre taken again, m_contactsChanged cleared.
  - StepWheel 0x004c94b0: on the ground (a spring force from the last step): rolling resistance
    against the motion, (g m) x (m_unknown594 / r), in proportion under 1 m/s, and friction against
    the slide of the point under the centre (x the sine, x the slide's speed under 1 m/s), with their

    place past the diameter, the step's sink and the contact range (types 0x8002, flags 0x210000):
    m_stopOnFlat stops the disc on ground flatter than 0.9; within the contact range the contact is the

  - C_Clock ("Clock", type 27, 0xE0 bytes, 0x005b8010..0x005b8860): StartMission(false) finds the
    hands "hour", "min", "sec" by their short names (_stricmp; the last match wins) and keeps their
    rotations; Update gathers the frame time (ms taken unsigned, * 0.001); with a second hand, once
    over a second has gathered (not at exactly 1), the hands turn about z from their start rotations
    by -6°, -0.1° and -1/120° per second of the whole time, and the gathered part keeps its fraction
    (quirk kept: without a second hand nothing turns). StopMission(false) puts them back. Neither
    activates the actor (only the module's RestartMission / LoadGame do). The saved game: the two
    floats raw; LoadGame turns the hands by Update(whole ms) and restores the fraction.
  - C_NullActor ("NullActor", type 33, 0x90 bytes): Activate / Deactivate, the base's save chunk.
  - C_WireCut ("WireCut", type 35, 0xB0 bytes): Cut(dir, hide) breaks the fence's door (Hit kind 3);
    false when it does not break; hides the broken part on request; in a local game joins the
    walking points on either side (found at StartMission: the nearest within 5 m of the frame, then
    the nearest to that point's mirror image) and gives them type 7 (CHECKPOINT_TYPE_FENCE_GAP, new);
    the clients get the heading (C_NetMessage::WriteDirection drops y), the frame's script OnUse(1).
    Clients break the door on the message / ReadState without the rest. StopMission resets the
    points' types. Saved game: chunk 1000 with the direction; LoadGame cuts again (the door breaks,
    the clients and the script are told again; quirk kept) and enters chunks of other ids without
    leaving them (quirk kept, as the animation actor's).
  - C_Switch ("Switch", type 13, 0x150 bytes): states 1..3 (others: 1), each a child frame named by
    its number. SetFrame counts the frames "1", "2", ... (Toggle wraps after the last; a fourth frame
    makes Toggle reach 4, clamped to 1) and puts each at "1"'s place under "1"'s parent. SetState
    tells the frame's script (a server, not quiet: RestartMission and LoadGame are quiet), relinks
    and shows the state's frame after hiding every child of the actor's frame, and plays the "sound"
    (object manipulation chapter, at the frame, silent to the AI). Its box volume (type 1,
    COLLISION_TYPE_SWITCH, new) on the server side or a pure client's. Properties: chunk 0 the state,
    1 the sound; WriteProperties returns false (quirk kept). Slots 0x24 / 0x28 save / read the state
    raw. SetFrame was split in Ghidra at 0x00613567: recreated as one function.
  - C_PhotoPlace ("Photo place", type 15, 0x140 bytes): StartMission once until StopMission: not

  - C_DocumentPlace ("Document place", type 23, 0x150 bytes): states 0 waiting (the name text

    documents' model, the item's, duplicated and left lying at the place, the frame hidden), 2 hidden,

    id 10 bits, the uid 16 bits). The original writes the kind with (int)(log(0) / log(2) + 1)
    bits: INT_MIN, which its bit stream masks to nothing and moves on by a word, so every message
    reads as a delivery (quirk kept: SetState's state message too). Fix: before that, its growing
    stream reserves 2^31 bits more (a 256 MB reallocation per message); the port writes the kind in
    a whole word, which keeps the wire layout. Fixes: no definition for the item type: text -1 and
    nothing shown (the original reads through null); the documents let go in the destructor.
    GetMaterialId 85.

    observer cursor, each pair also the other way round (quirk kept: the budget is charged for the

  PART 9b-iv-b DONE (2026-11-01): the paged text box's layout (src/game/paged_text_box.cpp;
  LayoutPages 0x006494a0 and 0x00649250..0x0064a6c0, 0x0064b7f0, 0x0064bad0, the empty FreePages
  0x0064ae60; 17 vector / word helpers absorbed, 0x0064a490..0x0064d050).
  - LayoutPages: false without runs or areas. Per run: its style (none: false); a first page; a
    STYLE_NEW_PAGE run when the last page has lines: the line being filled ends there, a new page;
    a line of its own, or joined to the last (a new line for the first after one of its own, or
    STYLE_NEW_LINE); the run flowed (false: the layout fails, the box stays dirty). Then the last
    line added, every page placed (PlacePages), the box clean.
  - The pages: NewPage in the next area in turn (the pages' count modulo the areas'), its room the
    area's height (truncated); AddLineToPage: to the last page, or a new one when its room is less
    than the line's height (gap + above + below; a page with less room than one line stays empty).
    A new line is as wide as the last page's area (when it was made: it keeps that width on a page
    of another area).
  - The flows (FlowPaged: UTF-8 with the unicode font, else bytes): measures from the style (size,
    above, below, rounded; not cut to the column); not wrapped: one word as wide as its text; else
    wrapped at the last blank that fits (a character fits under the room, not at it; a blank first
    in the text or after a wrap does not count), each full line added to the pages and a new one
    made. A word wider than a line: the part that fits; not a character on an empty line: false.
  - Placing: StackPage (the bases from the area's bottom, truncated, down by below + gap, then
    above; overflowing: the last base over the area's top); AlignPage / AlignPageLine (the first
    word's style: left, centred by half the room (truncated), right, justified when the next line,
    the next page's first too, starts justified (NextJustified: align bit 2): split at its blanks,
    the area's width shared out; a room of 0 or less: left; other aligns and STYLE_AT_X words not
    placed; no caret; a line's negative room sets m_overflows, never reset).
  - Quirks kept: the line's above and below take the style's below and above (the plain box's the
    other way round); FreePages empty: a layout again appends (the new lines to the last old page
    while it has room), every page placed again; a word wider than a line: by bytes its part has no
    width and the character that did not fit is dropped, by UTF-8 its part keeps its width and the
    character.
  - Fixes: a joined run right after a page break gets a new line (the original flows it into a
    null line); a failed layout deletes the line being filled (the original leaks it).
  - Harness: textbox_test.inc's "paged:" line in the text build (18 checks, the real layout; the
    9b-iv-a fake gone, its tests on the real layout). 22 of 22 in both orders; the camera and the
    world builds 22 of 22 in both orders.

  PART 10 PLAN (2026-11-05): the network module (0x00720000..0x0074a200, ~146 KB left). The game's own
  replication runs over DirectPlay8 (C_NetGame, 0x5080 bytes); the server browser's master-server code is the
  GameSpy SDK. Bottom-up:
  10a the replicated values' tracks (0x0073fbe0..0x00741530, 6 KB): the track base (vtable 0x00816490: a value
    interpolated towards the one received, its rate, the quantised delta sent per player), the position track
    (0x008164e0) and the direction track (0x00816530: yaw / pitch angles, the turn sign);
  10b the replicated objects (0x0073dd30..0x0073fbe0 and 0x00741530..0x00741700, 9 KB): their base (vtable
    0x00816438, the two quantisation triples), C_NetProperty (0x0081641c, 0x580 bytes: a position and a direction
    track, the last values sent to each of 32 players), C_NetObject (0x00816448: its properties, message queues and
    state handlers), C_NetReplication (0x0081646c: the objects, the local one, the view-cone filter 0x00816594),
    C_NetObjectMessage (0x00810630), S_PerfTimer;

    0x008165f0 .. 0x008166dc; split when started);
  10d.. C_NetGame (0x00720000..0x00733000, 69 KB: the DirectPlay8 interfaces declared by hand (the MSVC SDK has no
    dplay8.h), init / host / connect, the session queries, the messages, the players / teams / scores, the
    commands, the voice (0x00816598), the firewall and the CD key; split by subject when started);

    (0x007442e0..0x00745300).
  The GameSpy SDK (0x00733000..0x0073d260, 29 KB: the master-server list, the query / report protocol) is

Ghidra (2026-10-15): the dedicated server HD2DS_SabreSquadron.exe is open next to the client, so every
plugin request needs `program=HD2_SabreSquadron_unpacked.exe`; the tools import `tools/ghidra_program.py`
(patches urllib to add it; override with GHIDRA_PROGRAM).

Tier 1 spec types (2026-10-16): gen_tier1 takes them from `_re/ghidra_types.h`, which only a full
`apply_ghidra.py --types` used to rewrite (stale since 10-13). `import_spec.py` now rewrites it after each
import (`write_types_header()`). Specs are emitted in file order, so a spec must sort before the specs
that embed its structs by value (hence `35_collision.spec`).

Tier 1 thunks (2026-10-19): Ghidra shows a JMP thunk it has no own name for under its target's name,
so the export gives both one name and tier 1 defined it twice. 9 such redefinitions were left, all
lone `JMP` thunks (checked with `asm.py dis`) in front of the stl_idioms empty functions:
0x004c7d90, 0x004e2660, 0x0056a8a0, 0x0056a8c0, 0x00602b00, 0x00721f30, 0x0072d660, 0x007407c0,
0x00763930 -> empty_function_<next>. Instead of naming them in `names/` (zz_idioms.txt is regenerated
by stl_idioms.py, which only names `FUN_*` functions, so hand lines there would be lost),
`gen_tier1.find_thunks` now treats a 5-byte function that a function of the same name lists as a
caller as such a thunk and emits it as `<Target>Thunk`, the hand-naming convention (it skips the
thunk's aliases too). It finds 51 (14 emitted; the others or their targets are ported), e.g.
`C_Module__dtor_C_ModuleThunk` 0x004336b0, `C_Actor__CopyFromThunk` 0x005df3c0 and 0x00737cd0
`WSACleanupThunk` (whose callers now reach winsock's WSACleanup instead of the tier-1 thunk that
called itself). The Ghidra DB is unchanged; thunks named there (0x005d4a30) no longer match.
Tier 1 after the fix: 6599 functions, 811 errors in 128/591 files, 297 stubs (820 errors before; the
only difference is the 9 redefinitions).

Type-import safety (2026-10-13): `import_spec.py` now emits spec enums by name and takes `*_vtbl`
definitions from the generators; `import_spec.py --structs A_vtbl,...` repairs vtables. After any
import: fetch, diff against the snapshot, and check that no "/" struct named `_func_N` exists.
